  PRINTAP — PRIVACY-FIRST PEER-TO-PEER DOCUMENT PRINTING PLATFORM
  COMPREHENSIVE CODEBASE SPECIFICATION & FULL SOURCE CODE AUDIT
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Generated: 2026-10-19T11:37:03.194Z
Target Stack: Electron 33, React 19, TypeScript 5.7, Vite 5, Supabase (PostgreSQL 15), WebRTC DataChannel

───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
1. ARCHITECTURAL OVERVIEW & HIGH-LEVEL SYSTEM DESIGN
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Printap eliminates cloud storage risks for commercial document printing (Xerox / print shops).
In traditional print shops, customers email or WhatsApp sensitive PDFs (legal documents, Aadhaar cards,
academic certificates, medical records) to public computers where they persist unencrypted on disk.

Printap replaces this with a ZERO-CLOUD-STORAGE peer-to-peer streaming pipeline:
1. CUSTOMER EXPERIENCE (Web PWA):
   - The customer scans a dynamic counter QR code at the physical counter.
   - A client-side PWA opens in their mobile browser (no mobile app installation required).
   - The customer selects PDF/image documents and configures print parameters (color/BW, copies, duplex, page range).
   - Dynamic client-side pricing calculates costs in real-time according to the shop's active rate matrix.

2. PEER-TO-PEER DATA PIPELINE (WebRTC DataChannel):
   - Customer and Shop negotiate an encrypted WebRTC PeerConnection via Supabase Realtime Broadcast signaling.
   - Files are sliced into 64KB binary chunks and streamed DIRECTLY from the customer's mobile browser to the shop desktop terminal over an encrypted RTCDataChannel.
   - ZERO file bytes are ever uploaded to, or stored on, Supabase storage or cloud servers.
   - Event-driven DataChannel backpressure monitoring (1MB high-water threshold, 256KB low-water drain event) ensures reliable transmission without buffer overflow.
   - Contiguous pre-allocated zero-copy byte assembly on the desktop terminal prevents double memory allocation.

3. COUNTER TERMINAL AGENT (Electron Desktop App):
   - Runs locally on the shopkeeper's counter PC (Windows).
   - Automatically discovers physical hardware printers via native PowerShell/WMI and excludes virtual queues (PDF writers, OneNote, fax), with UTF-8 encoding and bounded timeouts.
   - Maps document print settings (B&W vs Color, A4 vs A3) to target physical printers.
   - Manages a local FIFO spooling queue with atomic Token verification and bounded memory retention (zeroing V8 ArrayBuffers upon completion/cancellation).
   - Streams PDF buffers directly into the Windows native print spooler (`pdf-to-printer`).
   - Implements SECURE SHREDDING (`secure-rm` DoD 5220.22-M 3-pass overwrite with unlinking fallback) immediately after two-phase spooler lock release.

4. TOKEN & ESCROW MONETIZATION ENGINE:
   - Each print transaction requires 1 Printap Token.
   - PostgreSQL RPCs (`reserve_shop_token`, `refund_shop_token`, `credit_shop_tokens`) enforce atomic token balance verification.
   - Pessimistic row-locking (`FOR UPDATE`) and unique partial constraints eliminate race conditions and double reservations/refunds.
   - Tokens are escrowed upon WebRTC handshake and atomically consumed only when the physical print job succeeds.
   - If the printer jams, runs out of paper, or the customer cancels, the escrowed token is instantly refunded back to the shop's balance.

FILE [2/46]: .github/workflows/release.yml
PURPOSE: GitHub Actions Windows Desktop Release & Auto-Updater Workflow
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
name: Release Desktop Agent

on:
  push:
    tags:
      - 'v*'

permissions:
  contents: write

jobs:
  build-and-release:
    runs-on: windows-latest

    steps:
      - name: Checkout Code
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Setup Node.js
        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: 20
          cache: 'npm'

      - name: Install Dependencies
        run: npm ci

      - name: Build Desktop App
        run: npm -w apps/desktop run build

      - name: Package Desktop App (NSIS)
        run: npx electron-builder --project apps/desktop --win --publish never

      - name: Clean up duplicate releases
        env:
          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
        run: node scripts/cleanup_releases.cjs

      - name: Publish Release to GitHub
        uses: softprops/action-gh-release@v2
        with:
          files: |
            apps/desktop/dist/*.exe
            apps/desktop/dist/*.blockmap
            apps/desktop/dist/latest.yml
          tag_name: ${{ github.ref_name }}
          name: Printap v${{ github.ref_name }}
          draft: false
          prerelease: false
          generate_release_notes: true
        env:
          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}

FILE [3/46]: supabase/schema.sql
PURPOSE: Core Supabase SQL Schema (Tables, RLS, Functions)
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- ============================================================
-- Printap — Supabase SQL Schema
-- Run this in the Supabase SQL Editor (Dashboard → SQL Editor)
-- ============================================================

-- 1. SHOPS TABLE
-- Stores each registered xerox shop.
CREATE TABLE public.shops (
  id          UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  name        TEXT NOT NULL,
  slug        TEXT UNIQUE,              -- e.g. "rahuls-xerox" for {slug}.mellod.in
  upi_vpa     TEXT NOT NULL,            -- e.g. "shop@upi"
  status      TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active'
                CHECK (status IN ('active', 'suspended')),
  created_at  TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

-- Index for quick status lookups (kill-switch check) and subdomain routing
CREATE INDEX idx_shops_status ON public.shops (status);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX idx_shops_slug ON public.shops (slug);

COMMENT ON TABLE  public.shops IS 'Registered xerox shops on the Printap platform.';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.shops.slug IS 'Unique URL-safe slug for subdomain routing ({slug}.mellod.in).';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.shops.upi_vpa IS 'UPI Virtual Payment Address for dynamic QR generation.';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.shops.status  IS 'active = accepting jobs, suspended = dues unpaid (kill-switch).';


-- 2. PRINT_EVENTS TABLE
-- One row per successful print session. Used for billing (₹1/print).
CREATE TABLE public.print_events (
  id          UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  shop_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.shops (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  session_id  TEXT NOT NULL,             -- correlates WebRTC session
  fee_amount  NUMERIC(6, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1.00,
  created_at  TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

-- Index for weekly invoice aggregation
CREATE INDEX idx_print_events_shop_created
  ON public.print_events (shop_id, created_at DESC);

COMMENT ON TABLE  public.print_events IS 'Ledger of completed print jobs; each row = ₹1 platform fee.';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.print_events.session_id IS 'Opaque session identifier from the WebRTC handshake.';


-- 3. ROW LEVEL SECURITY (RLS)
-- Enable RLS on both tables.
ALTER TABLE public.shops        ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE public.print_events ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- ---- SHOPS POLICIES ----

-- Anyone (anon) can READ a shop's public info (needed for the customer web app
-- to fetch shop name, UPI VPA, and status before initiating a session).
CREATE POLICY "shops_select_public"
  ON public.shops FOR SELECT
  USING (true);

-- Only service_role (backend/admin) can INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE shops.
-- (Shop registration is an admin operation, not done from the client.)
CREATE POLICY "shops_insert_service_role"
  ON public.shops FOR INSERT
  WITH CHECK (auth.role() = 'service_role');

CREATE POLICY "shops_update_service_role"
  ON public.shops FOR UPDATE
  USING (auth.role() = 'service_role')
  WITH CHECK (auth.role() = 'service_role');

CREATE POLICY "shops_delete_service_role"
  ON public.shops FOR DELETE
  USING (auth.role() = 'service_role');

-- ---- PRINT_EVENTS POLICIES ----

-- The Electron agent (authenticated as anon with shop_id context) can INSERT
-- print events. We allow anon inserts because the desktop agent logs events
-- after a successful print, and it uses the anon key.
CREATE POLICY "print_events_insert_anon"
  ON public.print_events FOR INSERT
  WITH CHECK (true);

-- Only service_role can SELECT print_events (for invoicing / admin dashboard).
CREATE POLICY "print_events_select_service_role"
  ON public.print_events FOR SELECT
  USING (auth.role() = 'service_role');

-- No UPDATE or DELETE on print_events (immutable ledger).


-- 4. REALTIME — enable the signaling channel
-- Supabase Realtime Broadcast doesn't need a table, but if you want to use
-- Realtime Postgres Changes on print_events (e.g. for a live dashboard):
ALTER PUBLICATION supabase_realtime ADD TABLE public.print_events;


-- 5. PRINTER ASSIGNMENTS TABLE
-- Maps OS printer names to roles (B&W, Color, Both) per shop.
CREATE TABLE public.printer_assignments (
  id            UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  shop_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.shops (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  printer_name  TEXT NOT NULL,
  role          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'bw'
                  CHECK (role IN ('bw', 'color', 'both')),
  is_default    BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  is_active     BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  created_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX idx_printer_assignments_unique
  ON public.printer_assignments (shop_id, printer_name);

ALTER TABLE public.printer_assignments ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Anon can read and write printer assignments (Electron agent uses anon key)
CREATE POLICY "printer_assignments_select_anon"
  ON public.printer_assignments FOR SELECT
  USING (true);

CREATE POLICY "printer_assignments_insert_anon"
  ON public.printer_assignments FOR INSERT
  WITH CHECK (true);

CREATE POLICY "printer_assignments_update_anon"
  ON public.printer_assignments FOR UPDATE
  USING (true)
  WITH CHECK (true);

CREATE POLICY "printer_assignments_delete_anon"
  ON public.printer_assignments FOR DELETE
  USING (true);

COMMENT ON TABLE  public.printer_assignments IS 'Maps OS printers to roles per shop.';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.printer_assignments.role IS 'bw = black & white, color = color, both = either.';


-- ============================================================
-- SEED DATA (optional — insert a test shop for development)
-- ============================================================
-- INSERT INTO public.shops (name, upi_vpa)
-- VALUES ('Dev Print Shop', 'devshop@upi');

FILE [4/46]: supabase/migrations/002_print_orders_and_queue.sql
PURPOSE: SQL Migration: Print Orders Table & Queue System
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- ============================================================
-- Migration: Add rate card, print orders, and queue system
-- Run in Supabase SQL Editor (Dashboard → SQL Editor)
-- ============================================================

-- 1. ADD RATE CARD COLUMNS TO SHOPS
ALTER TABLE public.shops
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS price_bw      NUMERIC(6, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 2.00,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS price_color   NUMERIC(6, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 10.00;

COMMENT ON COLUMN public.shops.price_bw    IS 'Price per B&W page in INR.';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.shops.price_color IS 'Price per color page in INR.';


-- 2. FINISHING SERVICES TABLE (per-shop custom pricing)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.finishing_services (
  id          UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  shop_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.shops (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  type        TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (type IN ('spiral_binding', 'corner_staple', 'hard_binding', 'lamination')),
  label       TEXT NOT NULL,
  price       NUMERIC(6, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  charge_unit TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'per_copy' CHECK (charge_unit IN ('per_copy', 'per_sheet')),
  enabled     BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  created_at  TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE (shop_id, type)
);

ALTER TABLE public.finishing_services ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "finishing_services_select_public"
  ON public.finishing_services FOR SELECT
  USING (true);

CREATE POLICY "finishing_services_manage_service_role"
  ON public.finishing_services FOR ALL
  USING (auth.role() = 'service_role')
  WITH CHECK (auth.role() = 'service_role');

COMMENT ON TABLE public.finishing_services IS 'Per-shop finishing/add-on services with custom pricing.';


-- 3. PRINT ORDERS TABLE (full order with multiple documents)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.print_orders (
  id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  shop_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.shops (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  session_id      TEXT NOT NULL,

  -- Token & Queue
  token_code      TEXT,            -- e.g. "A-14"
  pickup_pin      TEXT,            -- 4-digit PIN
  queue_position  INT,
  
  -- Payment
  payment_method  TEXT CHECK (payment_method IN ('upi', 'cash')),
  payment_status  TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending'
                    CHECK (payment_status IN ('pending', 'awaiting_cash_confirmation', 'confirmed', 'failed')),
  total_amount    NUMERIC(8, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,

  -- Job lifecycle
  status          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending_payment'
                    CHECK (status IN ('pending_payment', 'queued', 'printing', 'ready_for_pickup', 'picked_up', 'cancelled')),

  created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX idx_print_orders_shop_status
  ON public.print_orders (shop_id, status);
CREATE INDEX idx_print_orders_token
  ON public.print_orders (shop_id, token_code);

ALTER TABLE public.print_orders ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Customers (anon) can insert orders and read their own (by session_id)
CREATE POLICY "print_orders_insert_anon"
  ON public.print_orders FOR INSERT
  WITH CHECK (true);

CREATE POLICY "print_orders_select_anon"
  ON public.print_orders FOR SELECT
  USING (true);

CREATE POLICY "print_orders_update_anon"
  ON public.print_orders FOR UPDATE
  USING (true)
  WITH CHECK (true);

COMMENT ON TABLE public.print_orders IS 'Customer print orders with queue tokens and payment tracking.';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.print_orders.token_code IS 'Queue token display code, e.g. A-14.';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.print_orders.pickup_pin IS '4-digit PIN for secure document collection.';


-- 4. ORDER DOCUMENTS TABLE (per-file within an order)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.order_documents (
  id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  order_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.print_orders (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  file_name       TEXT NOT NULL,
  file_size       BIGINT NOT NULL,
  file_type       TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (file_type IN ('pdf', 'image')),
  page_count      INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,

  -- Print settings
  copies          INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  color_mode      TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'bw' CHECK (color_mode IN ('bw', 'color')),
  duplex          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'single' CHECK (duplex IN ('single', 'double')),
  pages_per_sheet INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (pages_per_sheet IN (1, 2, 4, 6, 9)),
  page_fit        TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'fit' CHECK (page_fit IN ('fit', 'fill')),
  orientation     TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'portrait' CHECK (orientation IN ('portrait', 'landscape')),

  created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX idx_order_documents_order
  ON public.order_documents (order_id);

ALTER TABLE public.order_documents ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "order_documents_insert_anon"
  ON public.order_documents FOR INSERT
  WITH CHECK (true);

CREATE POLICY "order_documents_select_anon"
  ON public.order_documents FOR SELECT
  USING (true);

COMMENT ON TABLE public.order_documents IS 'Individual documents within a print order, each with its own settings.';


-- 5. ENABLE REALTIME on new tables
ALTER PUBLICATION supabase_realtime ADD TABLE public.print_orders;


-- 6. AUTO-UPDATE updated_at trigger for print_orders
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_updated_at_column()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
  NEW.updated_at = now();
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ language 'plpgsql';

CREATE TRIGGER update_print_orders_updated_at
  BEFORE UPDATE ON public.print_orders
  FOR EACH ROW
  EXECUTE FUNCTION update_updated_at_column();

FILE [5/46]: supabase/migrations/003_shop_services_and_pricing.sql
PURPOSE: SQL Migration: Shop Services & Tier Pricing
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- ============================================================
-- Migration: Add custom services & dynamic pricing to shops
-- ============================================================

-- 1. Ensure price_bw and price_color exist with defaults
ALTER TABLE public.shops
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS price_bw NUMERIC(6, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 2.00,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS price_color NUMERIC(6, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 10.00;

-- 2. Add services JSONB column to shops table to store custom services & toggles
ALTER TABLE public.shops
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS services JSONB DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb;

COMMENT ON COLUMN public.shops.services IS 'Array of configured services, prices, units, and active toggles for this shop.';

-- 3. Update RLS policy to allow shopkeeper updates via anon or authenticated
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "shops_update_anon" ON public.shops;
CREATE POLICY "shops_update_anon"
  ON public.shops FOR UPDATE
  USING (true)
  WITH CHECK (true);


───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

PURPOSE: SQL Migration: Shop Online/Offline Presence Sync
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- ============================================================
-- Migration: Add online presence tracking to shops
-- ============================================================

-- 1. Add online presence tracking columns to shops table
ALTER TABLE public.shops 
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS is_online BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false;

-- 2. Index for quick online status lookups
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_shops_is_online ON public.shops (is_online);

-- 3. Add last_seen_at for stale presence cleanup
ALTER TABLE public.shops 
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS last_seen_at TIMESTAMPTZ;

-- 4. Enable Supabase Realtime for shops table so customers get instant status changes
DO $$
BEGIN
  ALTER PUBLICATION supabase_realtime ADD TABLE public.shops;
EXCEPTION
  WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL;
END $$;

-- 5. Helper function for sweeping stale online shops (called by cron or edge function)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.cleanup_stale_online_shops(stale_seconds INT DEFAULT 90)
RETURNS integer
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
AS $$
DECLARE
  affected_count integer;
BEGIN
  UPDATE public.shops
  SET is_online = false
  WHERE is_online = true
    AND last_seen_at < (now() - (stale_seconds || ' seconds')::interval);
  GET DIAGNOSTICS affected_count = ROW_COUNT;
  RETURN affected_count;
END;
$$;

-- 6. Comments
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.shops.is_online IS 'true when the desktop agent is running and owner has toggled online.';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.shops.last_seen_at IS 'Last heartbeat timestamp from the desktop agent, used for stale cleanup.';
COMMENT ON FUNCTION public.cleanup_stale_online_shops IS 'Sweeps shops marked is_online=true whose last_seen_at heartbeat is older than stale_seconds.';

-- ============================================================
-- Migration 008: Analytics & Monthly Archival Lifecycle System
-- Run in Supabase SQL Editor (Dashboard -> SQL Editor)
-- ============================================================

-- 1. SHOP DAILY ANALYTICS TABLE
-- Stores pre-aggregated daily summaries per shop.
-- Lightweight and fast; permanent store even after raw orders are purged.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.shop_daily_analytics (
  id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  shop_id             UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.shops(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  analytics_date      DATE NOT NULL,
  total_orders        INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  total_jobs          INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  bw_pages            INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  color_pages         INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  gross_revenue       NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  upi_revenue         NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  cash_revenue        NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  platform_fees       NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  gst_amount          NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  net_earnings        NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE(shop_id, analytics_date)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_shop_daily_analytics_shop_date 
  ON public.shop_daily_analytics(shop_id, analytics_date DESC);

-- 2. PLATFORM DAILY ANALYTICS TABLE
-- Stores network-wide daily summaries for executive dashboard.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.platform_daily_analytics (
  analytics_date           DATE PRIMARY KEY,
  active_shops_count       INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  total_orders             INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  total_jobs               INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  total_volume_inr         NUMERIC(12, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  total_platform_fees_inr  NUMERIC(12, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  total_gst_inr            NUMERIC(12, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  updated_at               TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

-- 3. ARCHIVE AUDIT LOG TABLE
-- Tracks local exports and purges conducted by the Electron Admin app.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.archive_audit_log (
  id                 UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  month_identifier   TEXT NOT NULL,              -- e.g. "2026-09"
  cutoff_timestamp   TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
  orders_purged      INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  events_purged      INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  total_amount_inr   NUMERIC(12, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  exported_by        TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'electron_admin',
  status             TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'completed',
  created_at         TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_archive_audit_log_month
  ON public.archive_audit_log(month_identifier DESC);

-- 4. RLS POLICIES
ALTER TABLE public.shop_daily_analytics ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE public.platform_daily_analytics ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE public.archive_audit_log ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Allow select for public / anon
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "shop_daily_analytics_select_all" ON public.shop_daily_analytics;
CREATE POLICY "shop_daily_analytics_select_all"
  ON public.shop_daily_analytics FOR SELECT
  USING (true);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "shop_daily_analytics_upsert_all" ON public.shop_daily_analytics;
CREATE POLICY "shop_daily_analytics_upsert_all"
  ON public.shop_daily_analytics FOR ALL
  USING (true)
  WITH CHECK (true);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "platform_daily_analytics_select_all" ON public.platform_daily_analytics;
CREATE POLICY "platform_daily_analytics_select_all"
  ON public.platform_daily_analytics FOR SELECT
  USING (true);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "platform_daily_analytics_upsert_all" ON public.platform_daily_analytics;
CREATE POLICY "platform_daily_analytics_upsert_all"
  ON public.platform_daily_analytics FOR ALL
  USING (true)
  WITH CHECK (true);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "archive_audit_log_all" ON public.archive_audit_log;
CREATE POLICY "archive_audit_log_all"
  ON public.archive_audit_log FOR ALL
  USING (true)
  WITH CHECK (true);

-- 5. RPC FUNCTION: SYNC AGGREGATE ANALYTICS
-- Pre-aggregates raw orders and events up to an optional cutoff into the daily tables.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.sync_aggregate_analytics(p_cutoff_timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NULL)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_cutoff TIMESTAMPTZ := COALESCE(p_cutoff_timestamp, now());
  v_orders_synced INT := 0;
  v_events_synced INT := 0;
BEGIN
  -- 1. Aggregate print_orders by shop and date
  INSERT INTO public.shop_daily_analytics (
    shop_id,
    analytics_date,
    total_orders,
    total_jobs,
    gross_revenue,
    upi_revenue,
    cash_revenue,
    platform_fees,
    gst_amount,
    net_earnings,
    updated_at
  )
  SELECT
    o.shop_id,
    DATE(o.created_at) AS analytics_date,
    COUNT(o.id) AS total_orders,
    COUNT(o.id) AS total_jobs,
    COALESCE(SUM(o.total_amount), 0.00) AS gross_revenue,
    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN o.payment_method = 'upi' THEN o.total_amount ELSE 0 END), 0.00) AS upi_revenue,
    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN o.payment_method = 'cash' THEN o.total_amount ELSE 0 END), 0.00) AS cash_revenue,
    COALESCE(SUM(COALESCE(s.platform_fee, 0.82)), 0.00) AS platform_fees,
    COALESCE(SUM(COALESCE(s.gst_amount, 0.18)), 0.00) AS gst_amount,
    COALESCE(SUM(o.total_amount) - SUM(COALESCE(s.total_charge, 1.00)), 0.00) AS net_earnings,
    now()
  FROM public.print_orders o
  LEFT JOIN public.shops s ON s.id = o.shop_id
  WHERE o.created_at <= v_cutoff
    AND o.payment_status = 'confirmed'
  GROUP BY o.shop_id, DATE(o.created_at)
  ON CONFLICT (shop_id, analytics_date) DO UPDATE SET
    total_orders  = EXCLUDED.total_orders,
    total_jobs    = GREATEST(public.shop_daily_analytics.total_jobs, EXCLUDED.total_jobs),
    gross_revenue = EXCLUDED.gross_revenue,
    upi_revenue   = EXCLUDED.upi_revenue,
    cash_revenue  = EXCLUDED.cash_revenue,
    platform_fees = EXCLUDED.platform_fees,
    gst_amount    = EXCLUDED.gst_amount,
    net_earnings  = EXCLUDED.net_earnings,
    updated_at    = now();

  GET DIAGNOSTICS v_orders_synced = ROW_COUNT;

  -- 2. Aggregate direct print_events where no orders existed
  INSERT INTO public.shop_daily_analytics (
    shop_id,
    analytics_date,
    total_jobs,
    platform_fees,
    gst_amount,
    updated_at
  )
  SELECT
    e.shop_id,
    DATE(e.created_at) AS analytics_date,
    COUNT(e.id) AS total_jobs,
    COALESCE(SUM(e.fee_amount * 0.82), 0.00) AS platform_fees,
    COALESCE(SUM(e.fee_amount * 0.18), 0.00) AS gst_amount,
    now()
  FROM public.print_events e
  WHERE e.created_at <= v_cutoff
  GROUP BY e.shop_id, DATE(e.created_at)
  ON CONFLICT (shop_id, analytics_date) DO UPDATE SET
    total_jobs    = GREATEST(public.shop_daily_analytics.total_jobs, EXCLUDED.total_jobs),
    platform_fees = GREATEST(public.shop_daily_analytics.platform_fees, EXCLUDED.platform_fees),
    gst_amount    = GREATEST(public.shop_daily_analytics.gst_amount, EXCLUDED.gst_amount),
    updated_at    = now();

  GET DIAGNOSTICS v_events_synced = ROW_COUNT;

  -- 3. Roll up into platform_daily_analytics
  INSERT INTO public.platform_daily_analytics (
    analytics_date,
    active_shops_count,
    total_orders,
    total_jobs,
    total_volume_inr,
    total_platform_fees_inr,
    total_gst_inr,
    updated_at
  )
  SELECT
    analytics_date,
    COUNT(DISTINCT shop_id) AS active_shops_count,
    SUM(total_orders) AS total_orders,
    SUM(total_jobs) AS total_jobs,
    SUM(gross_revenue) AS total_volume_inr,
    SUM(platform_fees) AS total_platform_fees_inr,
    SUM(gst_amount) AS total_gst_inr,
    now()
  FROM public.shop_daily_analytics
  WHERE analytics_date <= DATE(v_cutoff)
  GROUP BY analytics_date
  ON CONFLICT (analytics_date) DO UPDATE SET
    active_shops_count      = EXCLUDED.active_shops_count,
    total_orders            = EXCLUDED.total_orders,
    total_jobs              = EXCLUDED.total_jobs,
    total_volume_inr        = EXCLUDED.total_volume_inr,
    total_platform_fees_inr = EXCLUDED.total_platform_fees_inr,
    total_gst_inr           = EXCLUDED.total_gst_inr,
    updated_at              = now();

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'success', true,
    'orders_synced', v_orders_synced,
    'events_synced', v_events_synced,
    'cutoff', v_cutoff
  );
END;
$$;

-- 6. RPC FUNCTION: PURGE ARCHIVED MONTH DATA
-- Purges old raw orders and events safely AFTER ensuring analytics are aggregated.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.purge_archived_month_data(
  p_cutoff_timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ,
  p_month_label TEXT
)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_orders_purged INT := 0;
  v_events_purged INT := 0;
  v_total_amount NUMERIC(12, 2) := 0.00;
BEGIN
  -- 1. Ensure daily summaries are 100% computed prior to purge
  PERFORM public.sync_aggregate_analytics(p_cutoff_timestamp);

  -- 2. Calculate total amount about to be purged
  SELECT COALESCE(SUM(total_amount), 0.00), COUNT(*)
  INTO v_total_amount, v_orders_purged
  FROM public.print_orders
  WHERE created_at < p_cutoff_timestamp;

  -- 3. Count events
  SELECT COUNT(*)
  INTO v_events_purged
  FROM public.print_events
  WHERE created_at < p_cutoff_timestamp;

  -- 4. Purge raw print orders (cascade will purge order_documents)
  DELETE FROM public.print_orders
  WHERE created_at < p_cutoff_timestamp;

  -- 5. Purge raw print events
  DELETE FROM public.print_events
  WHERE created_at < p_cutoff_timestamp;

  -- 6. Record audit log
  INSERT INTO public.archive_audit_log (
    month_identifier,
    cutoff_timestamp,
    orders_purged,
    events_purged,
    total_amount_inr,
    exported_by,
    status
  ) VALUES (
    p_month_label,
    p_cutoff_timestamp,
    v_orders_purged,
    v_events_purged,
    v_total_amount,
    'electron_admin',
    'completed'
  );

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'success', true,
    'month', p_month_label,
    'cutoff', p_cutoff_timestamp,
    'orders_purged', v_orders_purged,
    'events_purged', v_events_purged,
    'total_amount_purged', v_total_amount
  );
END;
$$;

FILE [8/46]: supabase/migrations/009_shop_owner_auth_and_location.sql
PURPOSE: SQL Migration: Shop Owner Auth & Location Profiles
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- ============================================================
-- Migration: 009_shop_owner_auth_and_location.sql
-- Purpose: 
--   1. Add Owner Authentication (Email, Password Hash, Phone, Verification) to public.shops
--   2. Add Store Geolocation (Latitude, Longitude, Address, City, State, Pincode) to public.shops
--   3. Create public.email_otps table for Resend-powered 6-digit OTP verification
--   4. Enforce strict Row Level Security (RLS) on sensitive auth tokens
-- ============================================================

-- 1. ADD OWNER AUTH & LOCATION COLUMNS TO PUBLIC.SHOPS
ALTER TABLE public.shops
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS owner_name      TEXT,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS owner_email     TEXT,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS owner_phone     TEXT,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS password_hash   TEXT,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS email_verified  BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS latitude        NUMERIC(10, 7),
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS longitude       NUMERIC(10, 7),
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS address         TEXT,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS city            TEXT,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS state           TEXT,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pincode         TEXT;

-- 2. CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ON OWNER EMAIL (Case-insensitive)
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_shops_owner_email 
  ON public.shops (lower(owner_email)) 
  WHERE owner_email IS NOT NULL;

-- 2.1 ENFORCE VALID EMAIL FORMAT AT THE DATABASE LEVEL
DO $$
BEGIN
  IF NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint WHERE conname = 'check_owner_email_format'
  ) THEN
    ALTER TABLE public.shops
      ADD CONSTRAINT check_owner_email_format 
      CHECK (owner_email IS NULL OR owner_email ~* '^[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}$');
  END IF;
END $$;

-- 3. CREATE SPATIAL/GEOLOCATION INDEX FOR FAST NEARBY SHOP LOOKUPS
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_shops_location 
  ON public.shops (latitude, longitude) 
  WHERE latitude IS NOT NULL AND longitude IS NOT NULL;

-- 4. CREATE EMAIL OTPS TABLE FOR SECURE RESEND EMAIL VERIFICATION
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.email_otps (
  id            UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  email         TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (email ~* '^[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}$'),
  otp_hash      TEXT NOT NULL, -- SHA-256 hash of the 6-digit OTP code (never plaintext)
  purpose       TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (purpose IN ('onboarding', 'reset_password')),
  attempts      INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  max_attempts  INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 5,
  expires_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
  consumed      BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  created_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

-- Index for rapid OTP lookup and rate-limiting sweeps
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_email_otps_lookup 
  ON public.email_otps (email, purpose, consumed, expires_at);

-- 5. ROW LEVEL SECURITY (RLS) POLICIES
-- email_otps is strictly a server-side table (accessible ONLY by service_role)
ALTER TABLE public.email_otps ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "email_otps_service_role_all" ON public.email_otps;
CREATE POLICY "email_otps_service_role_all"
  ON public.email_otps
  FOR ALL
  USING (auth.role() = 'service_role')
  WITH CHECK (auth.role() = 'service_role');

-- Prevent public anon clients from reading password hashes or sensitive owner details
-- Existing public read policies on shops should only be allowed for non-sensitive storefront columns
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.shops.password_hash  IS 'Bcrypt-hashed owner password. NEVER exposed via public APIs.';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.shops.owner_email    IS 'Unique login email of the shop owner.';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.shops.latitude       IS 'Store location latitude selected on onboarding map.';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.shops.longitude      IS 'Store location longitude selected on onboarding map.';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.shops.address        IS 'Physical shop address or landmark.';
COMMENT ON TABLE public.email_otps            IS 'Stores SHA-256 hashed 6-digit OTPs for onboarding and password resets via Resend.';

FILE [9/46]: supabase/migrations/013_shop_tokens.sql
PURPOSE: SQL Migration: Token Ledger & Subscription Balance
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- ============================================================
-- Migration: Add token-based subscription model
-- ============================================================

-- 1. Add token balance and lifetime usage to shops table
ALTER TABLE public.shops
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS token_balance INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS tokens_used INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;

COMMENT ON COLUMN public.shops.token_balance IS 'Current available tokens (1 token = 1 successful print session).';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.shops.tokens_used IS 'Lifetime consumed tokens.';

-- 2. Create token_transactions audit log table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.token_transactions (
  id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  shop_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.shops (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  type            TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (type IN ('topup', 'deduction')),
  amount          INT NOT NULL,
  balance_after   INT NOT NULL,
  reference       TEXT,
  note            TEXT,
  created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX idx_token_transactions_shop 
  ON public.token_transactions (shop_id, created_at DESC);

ALTER TABLE public.token_transactions ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "token_transactions_select_service_role"
  ON public.token_transactions FOR SELECT
  USING (auth.role() = 'service_role');

CREATE POLICY "token_transactions_insert_service_role"
  ON public.token_transactions FOR INSERT
  WITH CHECK (auth.role() = 'service_role');

-- We also allow the desktop app (authenticated as anon) to insert deductions,
-- similar to how it logs print_events.
CREATE POLICY "token_transactions_insert_anon"
  ON public.token_transactions FOR INSERT
  WITH CHECK (true);

COMMENT ON TABLE public.token_transactions IS 'Immutable audit log for token top-ups and deductions.';

-- 3. Atomic RPC for token deduction
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.deduct_shop_token(
  p_shop_id UUID,
  p_session_id TEXT
) RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_current_balance INT;
  v_new_balance INT;
  C_WARN_THRESHOLD CONSTANT INT := 10;
BEGIN
  -- Atomic row-level lock to prevent race conditions
  SELECT token_balance
  INTO v_current_balance
  FROM public.shops
  WHERE id = p_shop_id
  FOR UPDATE;

  IF v_current_balance IS NULL THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'shop_not_found');
  END IF;

  IF v_current_balance <= 0 THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'insufficient_tokens', 'balance', 0);
  END IF;

  v_new_balance := v_current_balance - 1;

  -- Deduct
  UPDATE public.shops
  SET token_balance = v_new_balance,
      tokens_used = tokens_used + 1
  WHERE id = p_shop_id;

  -- Audit log
  INSERT INTO public.token_transactions (shop_id, type, amount, balance_after, reference, note)
  VALUES (p_shop_id, 'deduction', 1, v_new_balance, p_session_id, 'Print session completed');

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'success', true,
    'balance', v_new_balance,
    'alert', v_new_balance <= C_WARN_THRESHOLD
  );
END;
$$;

-- 4. Atomic RPC for token top-up
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.credit_shop_tokens(
  p_shop_id UUID,
  p_amount INT,
  p_reference TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
  p_note TEXT DEFAULT 'Manual top-up'
) RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_current_balance INT;
  v_new_balance INT;
BEGIN
  IF p_amount <= 0 THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'amount_must_be_positive');
  END IF;

  -- Atomic row-level lock
  SELECT token_balance
  INTO v_current_balance
  FROM public.shops
  WHERE id = p_shop_id
  FOR UPDATE;

  IF v_current_balance IS NULL THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'shop_not_found');
  END IF;

  v_new_balance := v_current_balance + p_amount;

  -- Credit
  UPDATE public.shops
  SET token_balance = v_new_balance
  WHERE id = p_shop_id;

  -- Audit log
  INSERT INTO public.token_transactions (shop_id, type, amount, balance_after, reference, note)
  VALUES (p_shop_id, 'topup', p_amount, v_new_balance, p_reference, p_note);

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'success', true,
    'balance', v_new_balance
  );
END;
$$;

FILE [10/46]: supabase/migrations/014_token_escrow_and_refunds.sql
PURPOSE: SQL Migration: Atomic Token Escrow & Automatic Refunds
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- ============================================================
-- Migration: 014_token_escrow_and_refunds.sql
-- Purpose:
--   1. Allow 'refund' type in token_transactions
--   2. Add escrow_session_id to token_transactions for pre-flight deduction tracking
--   3. Allow 'failed' and 'failed_refunded' in print_orders status
-- ============================================================

-- 1. Update token_transactions type check constraint
ALTER TABLE public.token_transactions DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS token_transactions_type_check;
ALTER TABLE public.token_transactions ADD CONSTRAINT token_transactions_type_check CHECK (type IN ('topup', 'deduction', 'refund'));

-- 2. Add escrow_session_id
ALTER TABLE public.token_transactions ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS escrow_session_id TEXT;
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_token_transactions_escrow ON public.token_transactions (escrow_session_id);

-- 3. Update print_orders status check constraint
ALTER TABLE public.print_orders DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS print_orders_status_check;
ALTER TABLE public.print_orders ADD CONSTRAINT print_orders_status_check 
  CHECK (status IN ('pending_payment', 'queued', 'printing', 'ready_for_pickup', 'picked_up', 'cancelled', 'failed', 'failed_refunded'));

-- 4. Create RPC for secure pre-flight token reservation (Escrow)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.reserve_shop_token(
  p_shop_id UUID,
  p_session_id TEXT
) RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_current_balance INT;
  v_new_balance INT;
  C_WARN_THRESHOLD CONSTANT INT := 10;
BEGIN
  -- Atomic row-level lock
  SELECT token_balance
  INTO v_current_balance
  FROM public.shops
  WHERE id = p_shop_id
  FOR UPDATE;

  IF v_current_balance IS NULL THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'shop_not_found');
  END IF;

  IF v_current_balance <= 0 THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'insufficient_tokens', 'balance', 0);
  END IF;

  -- Check if token is already reserved for this session (idempotency)
  IF EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.token_transactions 
    WHERE shop_id = p_shop_id AND escrow_session_id = p_session_id AND type = 'deduction'
  ) THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', true, 'balance', v_current_balance, 'status', 'already_reserved');
  END IF;

  v_new_balance := v_current_balance - 1;

  -- Deduct
  UPDATE public.shops
  SET token_balance = v_new_balance,
      tokens_used = tokens_used + 1
  WHERE id = p_shop_id;

  -- Audit log for escrow
  INSERT INTO public.token_transactions (shop_id, type, amount, balance_after, reference, note, escrow_session_id)
  VALUES (p_shop_id, 'deduction', 1, v_new_balance, p_session_id, 'Token reserved in escrow before print', p_session_id);

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'success', true,
    'balance', v_new_balance,
    'alert', v_new_balance <= C_WARN_THRESHOLD
  );
END;
$$;

-- 5. Create RPC for refunding token (used when customer confirms failure)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.refund_shop_token(
  p_shop_id UUID,
  p_session_id TEXT
) RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_current_balance INT;
  v_new_balance INT;
BEGIN
  -- Check if a deduction actually exists for this session
  IF NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.token_transactions 
    WHERE shop_id = p_shop_id AND escrow_session_id = p_session_id AND type = 'deduction'
  ) THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'no_reservation_found');
  END IF;

  -- Check if already refunded
  IF EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.token_transactions 
    WHERE shop_id = p_shop_id AND escrow_session_id = p_session_id AND type = 'refund'
  ) THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'already_refunded');
  END IF;

  -- Atomic row-level lock
  SELECT token_balance
  INTO v_current_balance
  FROM public.shops
  WHERE id = p_shop_id
  FOR UPDATE;

  IF v_current_balance IS NULL THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'shop_not_found');
  END IF;

  v_new_balance := v_current_balance + 1;

  -- Refund
  UPDATE public.shops
  SET token_balance = v_new_balance,
      tokens_used = GREATEST(0, tokens_used - 1)
  WHERE id = p_shop_id;

  -- Audit log for refund
  INSERT INTO public.token_transactions (shop_id, type, amount, balance_after, reference, note, escrow_session_id)
  VALUES (p_shop_id, 'refund', 1, v_new_balance, p_session_id, 'Token refunded due to print failure', p_session_id);

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'success', true,
    'balance', v_new_balance
  );
END;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.update_shop_settings(
  p_shop_id UUID,
  p_price_bw NUMERIC,
  p_price_color NUMERIC,
  p_services JSONB
) RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
BEGIN

    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'invalid_pricing');
  END IF;

  UPDATE public.shops
  SET price_bw = p_price_bw,
      price_color = p_price_color,
      services = COALESCE(p_services, services)
  WHERE id = p_shop_id;

  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'shop_not_found');
  END IF;

  RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', true);
END;
$$;

-- Controlled RPC for shop presence heartbeat (prevents anon from mutating token_balance or upi_vpa)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.set_shop_presence(
  p_shop_id UUID,
  p_is_online BOOLEAN

SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
BEGIN
  UPDATE public.shops
  SET is_online = p_is_online,
      last_seen_at = CASE WHEN p_is_online THEN now() ELSE last_seen_at END
  WHERE id = p_shop_id;

  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'shop_not_found');
  END IF;

  RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', true);
END;
$$;

-- 5. HARDEN TOKEN ESCROW, REFUND, AND CREDIT RPCs
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.reserve_shop_token(
  p_shop_id UUID,
  p_session_id TEXT
) RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_current_balance INT;
  v_new_balance INT;
  C_WARN_THRESHOLD CONSTANT INT := 10;
BEGIN
  IF p_shop_id IS NULL OR p_session_id IS NULL OR length(trim(p_session_id)) = 0 THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'invalid_parameters');
  END IF;

  -- 1. Acquire row-level lock on shop first to serialize all concurrent operations for this shop
  SELECT token_balance
  INTO v_current_balance
  FROM public.shops
  WHERE id = p_shop_id
  FOR UPDATE;

  IF v_current_balance IS NULL THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'shop_not_found');
  END IF;

  -- 2. Check idempotency under the shop lock
  IF EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.token_transactions
    WHERE shop_id = p_shop_id
      AND escrow_session_id = p_session_id
      AND type = 'deduction'
  ) THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', true, 'balance', v_current_balance, 'status', 'already_reserved');
  END IF;

  IF v_current_balance <= 0 THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'insufficient_tokens', 'balance', 0);
  END IF;

  v_new_balance := v_current_balance - 1;

  UPDATE public.shops
  SET token_balance = v_new_balance,
      tokens_used = tokens_used + 1
  WHERE id = p_shop_id;

  INSERT INTO public.token_transactions (
    shop_id, type, amount, balance_after, reference, note, escrow_session_id
  ) VALUES (
    p_shop_id, 'deduction', 1, v_new_balance, p_session_id, 'Token reserved in escrow before print', p_session_id
  );

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'success', true,
    'balance', v_new_balance,
    'alert', v_new_balance <= C_WARN_THRESHOLD
  );
EXCEPTION
  WHEN unique_violation THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', true, 'balance', v_current_balance, 'status', 'already_reserved');
END;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.refund_shop_token(
  p_shop_id UUID,
  p_session_id TEXT
) RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_current_balance INT;
  v_new_balance INT;
BEGIN
  IF p_shop_id IS NULL OR p_session_id IS NULL OR length(trim(p_session_id)) = 0 THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'invalid_parameters');
  END IF;

  -- 1. Lock shop row first to serialize concurrent refund attempts
  SELECT token_balance
  INTO v_current_balance
  FROM public.shops
  WHERE id = p_shop_id
  FOR UPDATE;

  IF v_current_balance IS NULL THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'shop_not_found');
  END IF;

  -- 2. Verify reservation exists
  IF NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.token_transactions
    WHERE shop_id = p_shop_id
      AND escrow_session_id = p_session_id
      AND type = 'deduction'
  ) THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'no_reservation_found');
  END IF;

  -- 3. Verify not already refunded
  IF EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.token_transactions
    WHERE shop_id = p_shop_id
      AND escrow_session_id = p_session_id
      AND type = 'refund'
  ) THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'already_refunded');
  END IF;

  v_new_balance := v_current_balance + 1;

  UPDATE public.shops
  SET token_balance = v_new_balance,
      tokens_used = GREATEST(0, tokens_used - 1)
  WHERE id = p_shop_id;

  INSERT INTO public.token_transactions (
    shop_id, type, amount, balance_after, reference, note, escrow_session_id
  ) VALUES (
    p_shop_id, 'refund', 1, v_new_balance, p_session_id, 'Token refunded due to print failure', p_session_id
  );

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'success', true,
    'balance', v_new_balance
  );
EXCEPTION
  WHEN unique_violation THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'already_refunded');
END;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.credit_shop_tokens(
  p_shop_id UUID,
  p_amount INT,
  p_reference TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
  p_note TEXT DEFAULT 'Manual top-up'
) RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_current_balance INT;
  v_new_balance INT;
BEGIN
  -- Restrict execution to service_role or verified platform_admins
  IF auth.role() <> 'service_role' AND NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.platform_admins WHERE id = auth.uid()
  ) THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'unauthorized_role');
  END IF;

  IF p_amount IS NULL OR p_amount <= 0 OR p_amount > 100000 THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'invalid_amount');
  END IF;

  SELECT token_balance
  INTO v_current_balance
  FROM public.shops
  WHERE id = p_shop_id
  FOR UPDATE;

  IF v_current_balance IS NULL THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'shop_not_found');
  END IF;

  v_new_balance := v_current_balance + p_amount;

  UPDATE public.shops
  SET token_balance = v_new_balance
  WHERE id = p_shop_id;

  INSERT INTO public.token_transactions (
    shop_id, type, amount, balance_after, reference, note
  ) VALUES (
    p_shop_id, 'topup', p_amount, v_new_balance, p_reference, p_note
  );

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'success', true,
    'balance', v_new_balance
  );
END;
$$;

COMMIT;


───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
FILE [13/46]: supabase/migrations/018_enterprise_lockdown.sql
PURPOSE: SQL Migration: Enterprise Security Lockdown & RPC Hardening
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- ============================================================
-- Migration: 018_enterprise_lockdown.sql
-- Purpose:
--   1. Drop legacy deduct_shop_token (superseded by reserve_shop_token)
--   2. Harden cleanup_stale_online_shops with explicit search_path
--   3. Restrict sync_aggregate_analytics and purge_archived_month_data
--      strictly to service_role or platform_admins
--   4. Provide controlled RPC for pruning removed printer_assignments
-- ============================================================

BEGIN;

-- 1. Drop legacy deduct_shop_token (superseded by reserve_shop_token)
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.deduct_shop_token(UUID, TEXT);

-- 2. Harden cleanup_stale_online_shops
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.cleanup_stale_online_shops(stale_seconds INT DEFAULT 90)
RETURNS integer
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  affected_count integer;
BEGIN
  UPDATE public.shops
  SET is_online = false
  WHERE is_online = true
    AND last_seen_at < (now() - (stale_seconds || ' seconds')::interval);
  GET DIAGNOSTICS affected_count = ROW_COUNT;
  RETURN affected_count;
END;
$$;

-- 3. Restrict sync_aggregate_analytics to service_role or platform_admins
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.sync_aggregate_analytics(p_cutoff_timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NULL)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_cutoff TIMESTAMPTZ := COALESCE(p_cutoff_timestamp, now());
  v_orders_synced INT := 0;
  v_events_synced INT := 0;
BEGIN
  IF auth.role() <> 'service_role' AND NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.platform_admins WHERE id = auth.uid()
  ) THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'unauthorized_role');
  END IF;

  INSERT INTO public.shop_daily_analytics (
    shop_id, analytics_date, total_orders, total_jobs,
    gross_revenue, upi_revenue, cash_revenue,
    platform_fees, gst_amount, net_earnings, updated_at
  )
  SELECT
    o.shop_id,
    DATE(o.created_at) AS analytics_date,
    COUNT(o.id) AS total_orders,
    COUNT(o.id) AS total_jobs,
    COALESCE(SUM(o.total_amount), 0.00) AS gross_revenue,
    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN o.payment_method = 'upi' THEN o.total_amount ELSE 0 END), 0.00) AS upi_revenue,
    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN o.payment_method = 'cash' THEN o.total_amount ELSE 0 END), 0.00) AS cash_revenue,
    COALESCE(SUM(COALESCE(s.platform_fee, 0.82)), 0.00) AS platform_fees,
    COALESCE(SUM(COALESCE(s.gst_amount, 0.18)), 0.00) AS gst_amount,
    COALESCE(SUM(o.total_amount) - SUM(COALESCE(s.total_charge, 1.00)), 0.00) AS net_earnings,
    now()
  FROM public.print_orders o
  LEFT JOIN public.shops s ON s.id = o.shop_id
  WHERE o.created_at <= v_cutoff
    AND o.payment_status = 'confirmed'
  GROUP BY o.shop_id, DATE(o.created_at)
  ON CONFLICT (shop_id, analytics_date) DO UPDATE SET
    total_orders  = EXCLUDED.total_orders,
    total_jobs    = GREATEST(public.shop_daily_analytics.total_jobs, EXCLUDED.total_jobs),
    gross_revenue = EXCLUDED.gross_revenue,
    upi_revenue   = EXCLUDED.upi_revenue,
    cash_revenue  = EXCLUDED.cash_revenue,
    platform_fees = EXCLUDED.platform_fees,
    gst_amount    = EXCLUDED.gst_amount,
    net_earnings  = EXCLUDED.net_earnings,
    updated_at    = now();

  GET DIAGNOSTICS v_orders_synced = ROW_COUNT;

  INSERT INTO public.shop_daily_analytics (
    shop_id, analytics_date, total_jobs, platform_fees, gst_amount, updated_at
  )
  SELECT
    e.shop_id,
    DATE(e.created_at) AS analytics_date,
    COUNT(e.id) AS total_jobs,
    COALESCE(SUM(e.fee_amount * 0.82), 0.00) AS platform_fees,
    COALESCE(SUM(e.fee_amount * 0.18), 0.00) AS gst_amount,
    now()
  FROM public.print_events e
  WHERE e.created_at <= v_cutoff
  GROUP BY e.shop_id, DATE(e.created_at)
  ON CONFLICT (shop_id, analytics_date) DO UPDATE SET
    total_jobs    = GREATEST(public.shop_daily_analytics.total_jobs, EXCLUDED.total_jobs),
    platform_fees = GREATEST(public.shop_daily_analytics.platform_fees, EXCLUDED.platform_fees),
    gst_amount    = GREATEST(public.shop_daily_analytics.gst_amount, EXCLUDED.gst_amount),
    updated_at    = now();

  GET DIAGNOSTICS v_events_synced = ROW_COUNT;

  INSERT INTO public.platform_daily_analytics (
    analytics_date, active_shops_count, total_orders, total_jobs,
    total_volume_inr, total_platform_fees_inr, total_gst_inr, updated_at
  )
  SELECT
    analytics_date,
    COUNT(DISTINCT shop_id) AS active_shops_count,
    SUM(total_orders) AS total_orders,
    SUM(total_jobs) AS total_jobs,
    SUM(gross_revenue) AS total_volume_inr,
    SUM(platform_fees) AS total_platform_fees_inr,
    SUM(gst_amount) AS total_gst_inr,
    now()
  FROM public.shop_daily_analytics
  WHERE analytics_date <= DATE(v_cutoff)
  GROUP BY analytics_date
  ON CONFLICT (analytics_date) DO UPDATE SET
    active_shops_count      = EXCLUDED.active_shops_count,
    total_orders            = EXCLUDED.total_orders,
    total_jobs              = EXCLUDED.total_jobs,
    total_volume_inr        = EXCLUDED.total_volume_inr,
    total_platform_fees_inr = EXCLUDED.total_platform_fees_inr,
    total_gst_inr           = EXCLUDED.total_gst_inr,
    updated_at              = now();

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'success', true,
    'orders_synced', v_orders_synced,
    'events_synced', v_events_synced,
    'cutoff', v_cutoff
  );
END;
$$;

-- 4. Restrict purge_archived_month_data strictly to service_role or platform_admins
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.purge_archived_month_data(
  p_cutoff_timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ,
  p_month_label TEXT
)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_orders_purged INT := 0;
  v_events_purged INT := 0;
  v_total_amount NUMERIC(12, 2) := 0.00;
BEGIN
  IF auth.role() <> 'service_role' AND NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.platform_admins WHERE id = auth.uid()
  ) THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'unauthorized_role');
  END IF;

  PERFORM public.sync_aggregate_analytics(p_cutoff_timestamp);

  SELECT COALESCE(SUM(total_amount), 0.00), COUNT(*)
  INTO v_total_amount, v_orders_purged
  FROM public.print_orders
  WHERE created_at < p_cutoff_timestamp;

  SELECT COUNT(*)
  INTO v_events_purged
  FROM public.print_events
  WHERE created_at < p_cutoff_timestamp;

  DELETE FROM public.print_orders
  WHERE created_at < p_cutoff_timestamp;

  DELETE FROM public.print_events
  WHERE created_at < p_cutoff_timestamp;

  INSERT INTO public.archive_audit_log (
    month_identifier, cutoff_timestamp, orders_purged, events_purged,
    total_amount_inr, exported_by, status
  ) VALUES (
    p_month_label, p_cutoff_timestamp, v_orders_purged, v_events_purged,
    v_total_amount, 'electron_admin', 'completed'
  );

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'success', true,
    'month', p_month_label,
    'cutoff', p_cutoff_timestamp,
    'orders_purged', v_orders_purged,
    'events_purged', v_events_purged,
    'total_amount_purged', v_total_amount
  );
END;
$$;

COMMIT;

FILE [16/46]: packages/shared/src/supabase.ts
PURPOSE: Shared Supabase Client, Domain Sanitizer, Types

import { createClient, SupabaseClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";

const getUrl = (): string => {
  // 1. Try Next.js process.env
  if (typeof process !== "undefined" && process.env?.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL) {
    return process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL;
  }
  if (typeof process !== "undefined" && process.env?.VITE_SUPABASE_URL) {
    return process.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL;
  }
  // 2. Try Vite import.meta.env (must be exactly import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL for static replacement)
  try {
    // @ts-ignore
    if (typeof import.meta !== "undefined" && import.meta.env && import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL) {
      // @ts-ignore
      return import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL;
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  
  return "";
};

const getKey = (): string => {
  // 1. Try Next.js process.env
  if (typeof process !== "undefined" && process.env?.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY) {
    return process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY;
  }
  if (typeof process !== "undefined" && process.env?.VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY) {
    return process.env.VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY;
  }
  // 2. Try Vite import.meta.env
  try {
    // @ts-ignore
    if (typeof import.meta !== "undefined" && import.meta.env && import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY) {
      // @ts-ignore
      return import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY;
    }
  } catch (e) {}

  return "";
};

const SUPABASE_URL = getUrl();
const SUPABASE_ANON_KEY = getKey();

let client: SupabaseClient | null = null;

/**
 * Returns a singleton Supabase client configured with the project's
 * URL and anon key. Safe to call from both browser and Node.
 */
export function getSupabase(): SupabaseClient {
  if (!client) {
    if (!SUPABASE_URL || !SUPABASE_ANON_KEY) {
      console.warn(
        "⚠️ [getSupabase] Warning: SUPABASE_URL or SUPABASE_ANON_KEY is not defined in current environment."
      );
    }
    client = createClient(SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_ANON_KEY, {
      realtime: {
        params: { eventsPerSecond: 20 },
      },
    });
  }
  return client;
}

PURPOSE: Shared Signaling Constants, Message Types, Payloads

import type { RealtimeChannel } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
import { getSupabase } from "./supabase";

export type SignalEvent =
  | "webrtc-offer"
  | "webrtc-answer"
  | "webrtc-ice"
  | "payment-confirmed"
  | "order-status"
  | "request-token"
  | "assign-token";

export interface SignalPayload {
  sessionId: string;
  data?: RTCSessionDescriptionInit | RTCIceCandidateInit | Record<string, unknown>;
  tokenCode?: string;
  queuePosition?: number;
  printerNumber?: number;
  paymentStatus?: string;
  status?: string;
  errorDetail?: string;
  confirmedAt?: string;
  hasColor?: boolean;
  totalAmount?: number;
  fileCount?: number;
}

export function channelName(shopId: string): string {
  return `signaling:${shopId}`;
}

const activeChannels = new Map<string, RealtimeChannel>();

/**
 * Cleanly removes and unsubscribes a cached signaling channel for `shopId`.
 */
export async function leaveSignalingChannel(shopId: string): Promise<void> {
  const existing = activeChannels.get(shopId);
  activeChannels.delete(shopId);
  if (existing) {
    try {
      const supabase = getSupabase();
      await existing.unsubscribe();
      await supabase.removeChannel(existing);
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn(`[signaling:${shopId}] Error removing channel:`, err);
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Joins or retrieves the cached signaling channel for `shopId`.
 * Guarantees a clean channel instance if `forceFresh` is true.
 */
export function joinSignalingChannel(
  shopId: string,
  forceFresh = false
): RealtimeChannel {
  const supabase = getSupabase();
  const existing = activeChannels.get(shopId);

  if (existing) {
    const state = existing.state;
    if (!forceFresh && (state === "joined" || state === "joining")) {
      return existing;
    }
    activeChannels.delete(shopId);
    try {
      existing.unsubscribe();
      supabase.removeChannel(existing);
    } catch {}
  }

  const channel = supabase.channel(channelName(shopId), {
    config: { broadcast: { self: false, ack: true } },
  });

  channel.subscribe((status) => {
    console.log(`[signaling:${shopId}] channel status → ${status}`);
    if (status === "CHANNEL_ERROR" || status === "TIMED_OUT" || status === "CLOSED") {
      if (activeChannels.get(shopId) === channel) {
        activeChannels.delete(shopId);
      }
    }
  });

  activeChannels.set(shopId, channel);
  return channel;
}

export async function waitForChannelReady(
  channel: RealtimeChannel,
  timeoutMs: number = 4000
): Promise<boolean> {
  if (channel.state === "joined") {
    return true;
  }
  if (channel.state === "closed" || channel.state === "errored") {
    return false;
  }

  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    let resolved = false;

    const done = (result: boolean) => {
      if (resolved) return;
      resolved = true;
      clearTimeout(timer);
      clearInterval(checkInterval);
      resolve(result);
    };

    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      done(channel.state === "joined");
    }, timeoutMs);

    const checkInterval = setInterval(() => {
      if (channel.state === "joined") {
        done(true);
      } else if (channel.state === "closed" || channel.state === "errored") {
        done(false);
      }
    }, 40);
  });
}

export async function broadcast(
  channel: RealtimeChannel,
  event: SignalEvent,
  payload: SignalPayload
): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    const isReady = await waitForChannelReady(channel, 2000);
    if (!isReady) {
      console.warn(`[signaling] Channel not ready for broadcast ${event}`);
      return false;
    }
    const res = await channel.send({
      type: "broadcast",
      event,
      payload,
    });
    if (res !== "ok") {
      console.warn(`[signaling] broadcast ${event} returned status:`, res);
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn(`[signaling] broadcast ${event} exception:`, err);
    return false;
  }
}

export const DEFAULT_ICE_SERVERS: RTCIceServer[] = [
  { urls: "stun:stun.l.google.com:19302" },
  { urls: "stun:stun1.l.google.com:19302" },
  { urls: "stun:stun2.l.google.com:19302" },
  { urls: "stun:stun.cloudflare.com:3478" },
  { urls: "stun:global.stun.twilio.com:3478" },
];

export async function waitForIceGathering(
  pc: RTCPeerConnection,
  maxWaitMs: number = 800
): Promise<void> {
  if (pc.iceGatheringState === "complete") {
    return;
  }

  await new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
    let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;

    const cleanup = () => {
      if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
      pc.removeEventListener("icegatheringstatechange", check);
    };

    const check = () => {
      if (pc.iceGatheringState === "complete") {
        cleanup();
        resolve();
      }
    };

    timer = setTimeout(() => {
      cleanup();
      resolve();
    }, maxWaitMs);

    pc.addEventListener("icegatheringstatechange", check);
  });
}
