import React, {
  createContext,
  useContext,
  useState,
  useEffect,
  useCallback,
} from "react";
import { getSupabase, sanitizeSubdomain, type ShopInfo } from "@printap/shared";

export type { ShopInfo };

export interface AuthContextType {
  isAuthenticated: boolean;
  shopId: string | null;
  shop: ShopInfo | null;
  isLoading: boolean;
  isGoogleSigningIn: boolean;
  login: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }>;
  loginWithGoogle: () => Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }>;
  cancelGoogleLogin: () => void;
  reopenGoogleBrowser: () => void;
  logout: () => void;
  refreshShop: () => Promise<void>;
  sendResetOtp: (email: string) => Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }>;
  resetPassword: (
    email: string,
    otp: string,
    newPassword: string
  ) => Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }>;
}

const STORAGE_KEY = "printap_shop_id";
const SAVED_EMAIL_KEY = "printap_saved_email";
const API_BASE_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || "https://www.mellod.in";

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | undefined>(undefined);

export function AuthProvider({
  children,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode;
}): React.ReactElement {
  const [shopId, setShopId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [shop, setShop] = useState<ShopInfo | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isGoogleSigningIn, setIsGoogleSigningIn] = useState(false);
  const activeGoogleSessionRef = React.useRef<{
    cancel: () => void;
    reopenBrowser: () => void;
  } | null>(null);

  const fetchShopDetails = useCallback(async (idOrSlug: string): Promise<ShopInfo | null> => {
    try {
      const supabase = getSupabase();
      const isUuid =
        /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-5][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(
          idOrSlug
        );

      let query = supabase.from("shops").select("*");
      if (isUuid) {
        query = query.eq("id", idOrSlug);
      } else {
        query = query.eq("slug", idOrSlug.toLowerCase());
      }

      const { data, error } = await query.maybeSingle();

      if (error || !data) {
        // Fallback: match by sanitized name if slug is not set
        const { data: allActive } = await supabase
          .from("shops")
          .select("*")
          .eq("status", "active");

        if (allActive && allActive.length > 0) {
          const match = allActive.find(
            (s) => sanitizeSubdomain(s.name) === sanitizeSubdomain(idOrSlug)
          );
          if (match) {
            const resolved = match as ShopInfo;
            if (!resolved.slug) resolved.slug = sanitizeSubdomain(resolved.name);
            return resolved;
          }
        }

        console.warn("[Auth] Failed to load shop:", error?.message);
        return null;
      }

      const resolvedShop = data as ShopInfo;
      // Auto-ensure slug is always populated from shop name
      if (!resolvedShop.slug && resolvedShop.name) {
        resolvedShop.slug = sanitizeSubdomain(resolvedShop.name);
      }

      return resolvedShop;
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("[Auth] Error fetching shop details:", err);
      return null;
    }
  }, []);

  // Restore saved shop from localStorage on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    const initAuth = async () => {
      const savedId = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
      if (savedId) {
        const shopDetails = await fetchShopDetails(savedId.trim());
        if (shopDetails && shopDetails.status !== "suspended") {
          setShopId(shopDetails.id);
          setShop(shopDetails);
        } else {
          localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
        }
      }
      setIsLoading(false);
    };

    initAuth();
  }, [fetchShopDetails]);

  const login = async (
    identifier: string,
    password: string
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> => {
    const cleanIdentifier = identifier.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!cleanIdentifier) {
      return { success: false, error: "Please enter your email or shop subdomain handle." };
    }
    if (!password) {
      return { success: false, error: "Please enter your password." };
    }

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/login`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ identifier: cleanIdentifier, password }),
      });

      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        if (data.unregistered) {
          const onboardUrl = `${API_BASE_URL}/onboard`;
          if (window.electronAPI?.openExternal) {
            window.electronAPI.openExternal(onboardUrl);
          } else {
            window.open(onboardUrl, "_blank");
          }
          return {
            success: false,
            error: "No registered shop found. Redirected to complete onboarding in your browser.",
          };
        }
        return { success: false, error: data.error || "Authentication failed." };
      }

      const shopDetails = data.shop as ShopInfo;
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, shopDetails.id);
      setShopId(shopDetails.id);
      setShop(shopDetails);
      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
      return { success: false, error: `Connection failed: ${msg}` };
    }
  };

  const sendResetOtp = async (
    email: string
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> => {
    const cleanEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!cleanEmail || !cleanEmail.includes("@")) {
      return { success: false, error: "Please enter a valid email address." };
    }

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/send-otp`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ email: cleanEmail, purpose: "reset_password" }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        return { success: false, error: data.error || "Failed to send reset code." };
      }
      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
      return { success: false, error: `Network error: ${msg}` };
    }
  };

  const resetPassword = async (
    email: string,
    otp: string,
    newPassword: string
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/reset-password`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          email: email.trim().toLowerCase(),
          otp: otp.trim(),
          newPassword,
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        return { success: false, error: data.error || "Password reset failed." };
      }
      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
      return { success: false, error: `Network error: ${msg}` };
    }
  };

  const loginWithGoogle = async (): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> => {
    const sessionId = crypto.randomUUID();
    setIsGoogleSigningIn(true);

    const authUrl = `${API_BASE_URL}/auth/desktop-login?session=${encodeURIComponent(sessionId)}`;

    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      let settled = false;

      const cleanup = () => {
        if (channel) {
          try {
            channel.unsubscribe();
          } catch {}
        }
        if (pollTimer) clearInterval(pollTimer);
        if (deepLinkCleanup) deepLinkCleanup();
        setIsGoogleSigningIn(false);
        activeGoogleSessionRef.current = null;
      };

      const onSuccess = async (shopData: ShopInfo) => {
        if (settled) return;
        settled = true;
        cleanup();
        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, shopData.id);
        setShopId(shopData.id);
        setShop(shopData);
        resolve({ success: true });
      };

      const onError = (error: string) => {
        if (settled) return;
        settled = true;
        cleanup();
        resolve({ success: false, error });
      };

      // 1. Listen via Supabase Realtime Broadcast Channel
      const supabase = getSupabase();
      const channel = supabase.channel(`desktop-auth:${sessionId}`, {
        config: { broadcast: { self: false } },
      });

      channel.on("broadcast", { event: "login-success" }, async ({ payload }) => {
        console.log("[Auth] Google login broadcast success:", payload);
        const p = payload as { shopId?: string; shop?: ShopInfo };
        if (p.shop) {
          onSuccess(p.shop);
        } else if (p.shopId) {
          const s = await fetchShopDetails(p.shopId);
          if (s) onSuccess(s);
          else onError("Failed to load shop details after login.");
        }
      });

      channel.on("broadcast", { event: "login-error" }, ({ payload }) => {
        const p = payload as { error?: string };
        onError(p.error || "Google authentication failed.");
      });

      channel.subscribe();

      // 2. Secondary Fallback: Poll desktop-session API every 1.5s
      const pollTimer = setInterval(async () => {
        if (settled) return;
        try {
          const res = await fetch(
            `${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/desktop-session?session=${encodeURIComponent(sessionId)}`
          );
          if (res.ok) {
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.status === "authenticated" && data.shop) {
              onSuccess(data.shop as ShopInfo);
            } else if (data.status === "unregistered") {
              onError(
                data.error ||
                  "No registered shop found. Redirected to complete onboarding in your browser."
              );
            }
          }
        } catch {}
      }, 1500);

      // 3. Tertiary: Deep-link listener from Electron main process
      let deepLinkCleanup: (() => void) | null = null;
      if (window.electronAPI?.onDeepLinkAuth) {
        deepLinkCleanup = window.electronAPI.onDeepLinkAuth(
          async ({ sessionId: linkSession, shopId: linkShopId }) => {
            if (linkSession === sessionId && linkShopId) {
              const s = await fetchShopDetails(linkShopId);
              if (s) onSuccess(s);
            }
          }
        );
      }

      // Open external system browser
      if (window.electronAPI?.openExternal) {
        window.electronAPI.openExternal(authUrl);
      } else {
        window.open(authUrl, "_blank");
      }

      // Store controller for cancellation or re-opening
      activeGoogleSessionRef.current = {
        cancel: () => {
          if (!settled) {
            settled = true;
            cleanup();
            resolve({ success: false, error: "Login cancelled by user." });
          }
        },
        reopenBrowser: () => {
          if (window.electronAPI?.openExternal) {
            window.electronAPI.openExternal(authUrl);
          } else {
            window.open(authUrl, "_blank");
          }
        },
      };
    });
  };

  const cancelGoogleLogin = () => {
    activeGoogleSessionRef.current?.cancel();
  };

  const reopenGoogleBrowser = () => {
    activeGoogleSessionRef.current?.reopenBrowser();
  };

  const logout = () => {
    localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    setShopId(null);
    setShop(null);
  };

  // Cancel active Google OAuth polling session on unmount
  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      activeGoogleSessionRef.current?.cancel();
    };
  }, []);

  const refreshShop = async () => {
    if (!shopId) return;
    const updated = await fetchShopDetails(shopId);
    if (updated) setShop(updated);
  };

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider
      value={{
        isAuthenticated: !!shopId && !!shop,
        shopId,
        shop,
        isLoading,
        isGoogleSigningIn,
        login,
        loginWithGoogle,
        cancelGoogleLogin,
        reopenGoogleBrowser,
        logout,
        refreshShop,
        sendResetOtp,
        resetPassword,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAuth(): AuthContextType {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useAuth must be used within an AuthProvider");
  }
  return context;
}


───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
FILE [28/46]: apps/desktop/src/renderer/lib/shop-signaling.ts
PURPOSE: WebRTC Receiver & Signaling Channel: SDP Handshake, Chunk Assembler
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
import {
  joinSignalingChannel,
  leaveSignalingChannel,
  broadcast,
  DEFAULT_ICE_SERVERS,
  waitForIceGathering,
  type SignalPayload,
  type PrintSettings,
  type TransferManifestOrder,
  type TransferFileHeaderMessage,
} from "@printap/shared";
import type { RealtimeChannel } from "@supabase/supabase-js";

export interface ShopSession {
  sessionId: string;
  pc: RTCPeerConnection;
  channel: RealtimeChannel;
  dataChannel: RTCDataChannel | null;
}

const MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES = 100 * 1024 * 1024; // 100MB hard cap per file
const MAX_ORDER_TOTAL_BYTES = 250 * 1024 * 1024; // 250MB hard cap per order
const PROGRESS_THROTTLE_MS = 100; // Max 10 UI updates per second

const sessions = new Map<string, ShopSession>();
const earlyCandidates = new Map<string, RTCIceCandidateInit[]>();
const earlyCandidateTimers = new Map<string, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>();
const sessionInactivityTimers = new Map<string, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>();

let activeSignalingShopId: string | null = null;

function resetSessionTimeout(sessionId: string, timeoutMs = 90_000): void {
  const existing = sessionInactivityTimers.get(sessionId);
  if (existing) clearTimeout(existing);
  sessionInactivityTimers.set(
    sessionId,
    setTimeout(() => {
      console.log(`[shop] Session ${sessionId} timed out due to inactivity`);
      cleanup(sessionId);
    }, timeoutMs)
  );
}

async function computeSha256Hex(buffer: ArrayBuffer): Promise<string> {
  const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", buffer);
  const hashArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer));
  return hashArray.map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}

export interface ReceivedOrderFile {
  fileId: string;
  fileName: string;
  fileSize: number;
  pageCount: number;
  settings: PrintSettings;
  buffer: ArrayBuffer;
}

export interface ReceivedOrder {
  sessionId: string;
  order: TransferManifestOrder;
  files: ReceivedOrderFile[];
}

export type FileReceivedCallback = (
  sessionId: string,
  fileBuffer: ArrayBuffer,
  fileName?: string
) => void;

export type OrderReceivedCallback = (order: ReceivedOrder) => void;

export interface SignalingCallbacks {
  onSessionStart?: (sessionId: string) => void;
  onRequestToken?: (
    sessionId: string,
    hasColor: boolean,
    totalAmount?: number,
    fileCount?: number
  ) => void;
  onFileProgress?: (
    sessionId: string,
    fileName: string,
    pct: number,
    received: number,
    total: number,
    fileIndex?: number,
    totalFiles?: number
  ) => void;
  onFileReceived: FileReceivedCallback;
  onManifestReceived?: (sessionId: string, order: TransferManifestOrder) => void;
  onOrderReceived?: OrderReceivedCallback;
  onSessionError?: (sessionId: string, error: string) => void;
}

export function startShopSignaling(
  shopId: string,
  callbacksOrHandler: FileReceivedCallback | SignalingCallbacks
): RealtimeChannel {
  const callbacks: SignalingCallbacks =
    typeof callbacksOrHandler === "function"
      ? { onFileReceived: callbacksOrHandler }
      : callbacksOrHandler;

  activeSignalingShopId = shopId;
  const channel = joinSignalingChannel(shopId, true);

  channel.on(
    "broadcast",
    { event: "webrtc-offer" },
    async ({ payload }: { payload: Record<string, unknown> }) => {
      const { sessionId, data } = payload as unknown as SignalPayload;
      if (!sessionId || !data) return;
      console.log(`[shop] Received offer for session ${sessionId}`);
      callbacks.onSessionStart?.(sessionId);
      await handleOffer(
        channel,
        sessionId,
        data as RTCSessionDescriptionInit,
        callbacks
      );
    }
  );

  channel.on(
    "broadcast",
    { event: "webrtc-ice" },
    async ({ payload }: { payload: Record<string, unknown> }) => {
      const { sessionId, data } = payload as unknown as SignalPayload;
      const candidateInit = data as RTCIceCandidateInit;
      if (!sessionId || !candidateInit || !candidateInit.candidate) return;
      const session = sessions.get(sessionId);

      if (!session || !session.pc.remoteDescription) {
        if (!earlyCandidates.has(sessionId)) {
          earlyCandidates.set(sessionId, []);
        }
        const queue = earlyCandidates.get(sessionId)!;
        if (queue.length < 50) {
          queue.push(candidateInit);
        }
        if (!earlyCandidateTimers.has(sessionId)) {
          earlyCandidateTimers.set(
            sessionId,
            setTimeout(() => {
              earlyCandidates.delete(sessionId);
              earlyCandidateTimers.delete(sessionId);
            }, 60_000)
          );
        }
        return;
      }

      try {
        await session.pc.addIceCandidate(new RTCIceCandidate(candidateInit));
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(`[shop] Failed to add ICE candidate:`, err);
      }
    }
  );

  channel.on(
    "broadcast",
    { event: "request-token" },
    ({ payload }: { payload: Record<string, unknown> }) => {
      const { sessionId, hasColor, totalAmount, fileCount } =
        payload as unknown as SignalPayload;
      if (!sessionId) return;
      callbacks.onRequestToken?.(
        sessionId,
        Boolean(hasColor),
        totalAmount,
        fileCount
      );
    }
  );

  channel.on(
    "broadcast",
    { event: "order-status" },
    ({ payload }: { payload: Record<string, unknown> }) => {
      const { sessionId, status, errorDetail } =
        payload as unknown as SignalPayload;
      if (!sessionId) return;
      if (status === "cancelled" || status === "error") {
        callbacks.onSessionError?.(
          sessionId,
          errorDetail || `Customer order was ${status}`
        );
        cleanup(sessionId);
      }
    }
  );

  console.log(`[shop] Signaling started for shop ${shopId}`);
  return channel;
}

export function stopShopSignaling(channel: RealtimeChannel | null): void {
  if (activeSignalingShopId) {
    leaveSignalingChannel(activeSignalingShopId).catch(() => {});
    activeSignalingShopId = null;
  } else if (channel) {
    try {
      channel.unsubscribe();
    } catch {}
  }

  for (const timer of earlyCandidateTimers.values()) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
  earlyCandidateTimers.clear();
  earlyCandidates.clear();

  for (const timer of sessionInactivityTimers.values()) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
  sessionInactivityTimers.clear();

  for (const sessionId of Array.from(sessions.keys())) {
    cleanup(sessionId);
  }
}

async function handleOffer(
  channel: RealtimeChannel,
  sessionId: string,
  offer: RTCSessionDescriptionInit,
  callbacks: SignalingCallbacks
): Promise<void> {
  const existing = sessions.get(sessionId);
  if (existing) {
    if (
      existing.pc.connectionState === "connected" ||
      existing.pc.connectionState === "connecting" ||
      existing.pc.signalingState === "stable"
    ) {
      if (existing.pc.localDescription) {
        const localDesc = existing.pc.localDescription;
        await broadcast(channel, "webrtc-answer", {
          sessionId,
          data: { type: localDesc.type, sdp: localDesc.sdp },
        });
      }
      return;
    } else {
      cleanup(sessionId);
    }
  }

  resetSessionTimeout(sessionId);

  const pc = new RTCPeerConnection({
    iceServers: DEFAULT_ICE_SERVERS,
    iceCandidatePoolSize: 10,
  });

  const session: ShopSession = {
    sessionId,
    pc,
    channel,
    dataChannel: null,
  };
  sessions.set(sessionId, session);

  pc.onicecandidate = (event) => {
    if (event.candidate && event.candidate.candidate) {
      broadcast(channel, "webrtc-ice", {
        sessionId,
        data: event.candidate.toJSON(),
      });
    }
  };

  pc.onconnectionstatechange = () => {
    if (pc.connectionState === "failed" || pc.connectionState === "closed") {
      callbacks.onSessionError?.(
        sessionId,
        "WebRTC peer connection closed or failed"
      );
      cleanup(sessionId);
    }
  };

  pc.ondatachannel = (event) => {
    session.dataChannel = event.channel;
    setupDataChannelReceiver(session, callbacks);
  };

  try {
    await pc.setRemoteDescription(new RTCSessionDescription(offer));

    const buffered = earlyCandidates.get(sessionId);
    if (buffered && buffered.length > 0) {
      for (const cand of buffered) {
        try {
          if (cand && cand.candidate) {
            await pc.addIceCandidate(new RTCIceCandidate(cand));
          }
        } catch {}
      }
      earlyCandidates.delete(sessionId);
    }

    const answer = await pc.createAnswer();
    await pc.setLocalDescription(answer);
    await waitForIceGathering(pc, 800);

    const localAnswer = pc.localDescription || answer;
    await broadcast(channel, "webrtc-answer", {
      sessionId,
      data: { type: localAnswer.type, sdp: localAnswer.sdp },
    });
  } catch (err) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
    callbacks.onSessionError?.(sessionId, msg);
    cleanup(sessionId);
  }
}

function setupDataChannelReceiver(
  session: ShopSession,
  callbacks: SignalingCallbacks
): void {
  const dc = session.dataChannel!;
  dc.binaryType = "arraybuffer";

  let manifest: TransferManifestOrder | null = null;
  const receivedFiles: ReceivedOrderFile[] = [];
  let orderTotalBytesReceived = 0;

  let currentFileHeader: TransferFileHeaderMessage | null = null;
  let legacyMetadata: { fileName: string; fileSize: number; totalChunks: number } | null = null;

  let assemblyBuffer: Uint8Array | null = null;
  let expectedSize = 0;
  let receivedSize = 0;
  let lastProgressEmitTime = 0;

  const finalizeCurrentFile = (): ArrayBuffer | null => {
    if (!assemblyBuffer || receivedSize === 0) return null;
    const rawBuffer = assemblyBuffer.buffer as ArrayBuffer;
    const finalBuffer =
      receivedSize === assemblyBuffer.byteLength
        ? rawBuffer
        : rawBuffer.slice(0, receivedSize);
    assemblyBuffer = null;
    return finalBuffer;
  };

  dc.onmessage = async (event) => {
    resetSessionTimeout(session.sessionId);

    if (typeof event.data === "string") {
      if (event.data === "EOF") {
        const finalBuf = finalizeCurrentFile();
        if (finalBuf) {
          if (currentFileHeader && (currentFileHeader as any).sha256) {
            const actualHash = await computeSha256Hex(finalBuf);
            if (actualHash !== (currentFileHeader as any).sha256) {
              callbacks.onSessionError?.(
                session.sessionId,
                `Integrity check failed for ${currentFileHeader.fileName}`
              );
              cleanup(session.sessionId);
              return;
            }
          }

          const fileName =
            currentFileHeader?.fileName ||
            legacyMetadata?.fileName ||
            `file_${receivedFiles.length + 1}`;

          if (currentFileHeader) {
            const alreadyPushed = receivedFiles.some(
              (f) => f.fileId === currentFileHeader!.fileId
            );
            if (!alreadyPushed) {
              receivedFiles.push({
                fileId: currentFileHeader.fileId,
                fileName: currentFileHeader.fileName,
                fileSize: finalBuf.byteLength,
                pageCount: currentFileHeader.pageCount,
                settings: currentFileHeader.settings,
                buffer: finalBuf,
              });
            }
          }

          if (!manifest) {
            callbacks.onFileReceived(session.sessionId, finalBuf, fileName);
          }

          receivedSize = 0;
          expectedSize = 0;
          legacyMetadata = null;
        }
        return;
      }

      try {
        const msg = JSON.parse(event.data);

        if (msg.type === "order-manifest") {
          manifest = msg.order;
          callbacks.onManifestReceived?.(session.sessionId, msg.order);
          return;
        }

        if (msg.type === "file-header") {
          if (
            typeof msg.fileSize !== "number" ||
            msg.fileSize <= 0 ||
            msg.fileSize > MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES
          ) {
            callbacks.onSessionError?.(
              session.sessionId,
              "Security Exception: File size invalid or exceeds 100MB limit."
            );
            cleanup(session.sessionId);
            return;
          }

          currentFileHeader = msg as TransferFileHeaderMessage;
          expectedSize = currentFileHeader.fileSize;
          receivedSize = 0;
          assemblyBuffer = new Uint8Array(expectedSize);
          lastProgressEmitTime = 0;

          callbacks.onFileProgress?.(
            session.sessionId,
            currentFileHeader.fileName,
            0,
            0,
            expectedSize,
            receivedFiles.length + 1,
            manifest?.totalFiles ?? 1
          );
          return;
        }

        if (msg.type === "file-eof") {
          const finalBuf = finalizeCurrentFile();
          if (currentFileHeader && finalBuf) {
            if ((currentFileHeader as any).sha256) {
              const actualHash = await computeSha256Hex(finalBuf);
              if (actualHash !== (currentFileHeader as any).sha256) {
                callbacks.onSessionError?.(
                  session.sessionId,
                  `Integrity check failed for ${currentFileHeader.fileName}`
                );
                cleanup(session.sessionId);
                return;
              }
            }

            const alreadyPushed = receivedFiles.some(
              (f) => f.fileId === currentFileHeader!.fileId
            );
            if (!alreadyPushed) {
              receivedFiles.push({
                fileId: currentFileHeader.fileId,
                fileName: currentFileHeader.fileName,
                fileSize: finalBuf.byteLength,
                pageCount: currentFileHeader.pageCount,
                settings: currentFileHeader.settings,
                buffer: finalBuf,
              });
            }

            if (!manifest) {
              callbacks.onFileReceived(
                session.sessionId,
                finalBuf,
                currentFileHeader.fileName
              );
            }
          }
          receivedSize = 0;
          expectedSize = 0;
          return;
        }

        if (msg.type === "order-complete") {
          if (manifest && callbacks.onOrderReceived) {
            callbacks.onOrderReceived({
              sessionId: session.sessionId,
              order: manifest,
              files: receivedFiles,
            });
          }
          return;
        }

        if (msg.fileName && typeof msg.fileSize === "number") {
          if (msg.fileSize <= 0 || msg.fileSize > MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES) {
            callbacks.onSessionError?.(
              session.sessionId,
              "Security Exception: File size exceeds 100MB limit."
            );
            cleanup(session.sessionId);
            return;
          }

          legacyMetadata = msg;
          expectedSize = msg.fileSize;
          receivedSize = 0;
          assemblyBuffer = new Uint8Array(expectedSize);
          lastProgressEmitTime = 0;

          callbacks.onFileProgress?.(
            session.sessionId,
            msg.fileName,
            0,
            0,
            expectedSize,
            1,
            1
          );
          return;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(`[shop] Failed to parse message on DataChannel:`, err);
      }
      return;
    }

    const chunk = event.data as ArrayBuffer;
    const chunkBytes = chunk.byteLength;

    orderTotalBytesReceived += chunkBytes;
    if (
      !assemblyBuffer ||
      receivedSize + chunkBytes > expectedSize ||
      orderTotalBytesReceived > MAX_ORDER_TOTAL_BYTES
    ) {
      callbacks.onSessionError?.(
        session.sessionId,
        "Security Exception: Binary stream overflowed declared file header size."
      );
      assemblyBuffer = null;
      cleanup(session.sessionId);
      return;
    }

    assemblyBuffer.set(new Uint8Array(chunk), receivedSize);
    receivedSize += chunkBytes;

    const now = performance.now();
    if (
      expectedSize > 0 &&
      (now - lastProgressEmitTime >= PROGRESS_THROTTLE_MS ||
        receivedSize === expectedSize)
    ) {
      lastProgressEmitTime = now;
      const currentName =
        currentFileHeader?.fileName ||
        legacyMetadata?.fileName ||
        "receiving...";
      const pct = Math.min(100, Math.round((receivedSize / expectedSize) * 100));
      callbacks.onFileProgress?.(
        session.sessionId,
        currentName,
        pct,
        receivedSize,
        expectedSize,
        receivedFiles.length + 1,
        manifest?.totalFiles ?? 1
      );
    }
  };

  dc.onclose = () => {
    assemblyBuffer = null;
    setTimeout(() => {
      cleanup(session.sessionId);
    }, 5000);
  };

  dc.onerror = () => {
    assemblyBuffer = null;
    callbacks.onSessionError?.(
      session.sessionId,
      "DataChannel encountered an error"
    );
  };
}

function cleanup(sessionId: string): void {
  const earlyTimer = earlyCandidateTimers.get(sessionId);
  if (earlyTimer) {
    clearTimeout(earlyTimer);
    earlyCandidateTimers.delete(sessionId);
  }
  earlyCandidates.delete(sessionId);

  const inactivityTimer = sessionInactivityTimers.get(sessionId);
  if (inactivityTimer) {
    clearTimeout(inactivityTimer);
    sessionInactivityTimers.delete(sessionId);
  }

  const session = sessions.get(sessionId);
  if (!session) return;

  try {
    session.dataChannel?.close();
    session.pc.close();
  } catch {}
  sessions.delete(sessionId);
}

export async function broadcastPaymentConfirmed(
  channel: RealtimeChannel,
  sessionId: string,
  tokenCode?: string
): Promise<void> {
  await broadcast(channel, "payment-confirmed", {
    sessionId,
    tokenCode,
    confirmedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  });
}

export async function broadcastOrderStatus(
  channel: RealtimeChannel,
  payload: {
    sessionId: string;
    tokenCode?: string;
    queuePosition?: number;
    printerNumber?: number;
    paymentStatus?: string;
    status?: string;
    errorDetail?: string;
  }
): Promise<void> {
  await broadcast(channel, "order-status", payload);
}

export async function broadcastTokenAssigned(
  channel: RealtimeChannel,
  payload: {
    sessionId: string;
    tokenCode: string;
    queuePosition?: number;
    printerNumber?: number;
  }
): Promise<void> {
  await broadcast(channel, "assign-token", payload);
}


───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
FILE [29/46]: apps/desktop/src/renderer/lib/shop-presence.ts
PURPOSE: Supabase Realtime Shop Presence & Heartbeat Engine
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
import {
  getSupabase,
  presenceChannelName,
  setShopOnline,
  HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_MS,
} from "@printap/shared";
import type { RealtimeChannel } from "@supabase/supabase-js";

let currentChannel: RealtimeChannel | null = null;
let currentShopId: string | null = null;
let heartbeatTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
let isRunning = false;
let transitionPromise: Promise<void> | null = null;

export type PresenceConnectionStatus =
  | "disconnected"
  | "connecting"
  | "online"
  | "error";

type StatusListener = (status: PresenceConnectionStatus, detail?: string) => void;
const listeners = new Set<StatusListener>();

export function subscribePresenceStatus(listener: StatusListener): () => void {
  listeners.add(listener);
  return () => {
    listeners.delete(listener);
  };
}

function notifyStatus(status: PresenceConnectionStatus, detail?: string) {
  for (const listener of listeners) {
    try {
      listener(status, detail);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("[presence] Listener error:", e);
    }
  }
}

export function isPresenceActive(): boolean {
  return isRunning && currentChannel !== null;
}

export function getCurrentPresenceShopId(): string | null {
  return currentShopId;
}

export async function startPresence(
  shopId: string,
  onStatusChange?: StatusListener
): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> {
  if (onStatusChange) {
    listeners.add(onStatusChange);
  }

  if (transitionPromise) {
    await transitionPromise;
  }

  if (isRunning && currentShopId === shopId && currentChannel) {
    return { success: true };
  }

  if (isRunning) {
    await stopPresence();
  }

  currentShopId = shopId;
  isRunning = true;
  notifyStatus("connecting", "Connecting to presence channel...");

  try {
    if (window.electronAPI?.setActiveShopId) {
      await window.electronAPI.setActiveShopId(shopId);
    }

    const dbResult = await setShopOnline(shopId, true);
    if (!dbResult.success) {
      console.warn("[presence] Initial DB mark online warning:", dbResult.error);
    }

    const supabase = getSupabase();
    const chName = presenceChannelName(shopId);

    const channel = supabase.channel(chName, {
      config: {
        presence: {
          key: shopId,
        },
      },
    });

    currentChannel = channel;

    channel
      .on("presence", { event: "sync" }, () => {
        console.log(`[presence] Channel synced for shop ${shopId}`);
      })
      .subscribe(async (status) => {
        if (status === "SUBSCRIBED") {
          try {
            await channel.track({
              shopId,
              timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
              isOnline: true,
            });
            notifyStatus("online", "Shop is online and broadcasting presence");
          } catch (trackErr) {
            console.error("[presence] Failed to track presence:", trackErr);
            notifyStatus("online", "Subscribed with tracking warning");
          }
        } else if (status === "CHANNEL_ERROR" || status === "TIMED_OUT") {
          notifyStatus("error", `Presence channel status: ${status}`);
        }
      });

    if (heartbeatTimer) clearInterval(heartbeatTimer);
    heartbeatTimer = setInterval(async () => {
      if (!isRunning || !currentShopId) return;
      try {
        if (currentChannel && currentChannel.state === "joined") {
          await currentChannel.track({
            shopId: currentShopId,
            timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
            isOnline: true,
          });
        }
        await setShopOnline(currentShopId, true);
      } catch (hbErr) {
        console.warn("[presence] Heartbeat tick error:", hbErr);
      }
    }, HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_MS);

    return { success: true };
  } catch (err) {
    const errorMsg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
    isRunning = false;
    notifyStatus("error", errorMsg);
    return { success: false, error: errorMsg };
  }
}

export async function stopPresence(): Promise<void> {
  if (transitionPromise) {
    await transitionPromise;
  }

  transitionPromise = (async () => {
    const shopId = currentShopId;
    isRunning = false;
    currentShopId = null;

    if (heartbeatTimer) {
      clearInterval(heartbeatTimer);
      heartbeatTimer = null;
    }

    if (currentChannel) {
      const ch = currentChannel;
      currentChannel = null;
      try {
        await ch.untrack();
        await ch.unsubscribe();
        const supabase = getSupabase();
        await supabase.removeChannel(ch);
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn("[presence] Error closing presence channel:", e);
      }
    }

    if (shopId) {
      try {
        if (window.electronAPI?.setActiveShopId) {
          await window.electronAPI.setActiveShopId(null);
        }
        await setShopOnline(shopId, false);
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn("[presence] Error setting shop offline in DB:", e);
      }
    }

    notifyStatus("disconnected", "Shop is offline");
  })();

  try {
    await transitionPromise;
  } finally {
    transitionPromise = null;
  }
}


───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
FILE [30/46]: apps/desktop/src/renderer/lib/fifo-dispatcher.ts
PURPOSE: FIFO Print Dispatcher: Queue Scheduler, Token Allocation, Hardware Spooler
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
import type { PrintSettings } from "@printap/shared";
import type { PrinterAssignment } from "../components/PrintersControl";

export interface ReceivedJobFile {
  fileId?: string;
  fileName: string;
  fileSize: number;
  pageCount: number;
  settings: PrintSettings;
  buffer: ArrayBuffer;
}

export interface QueuedPrintJob {
  sessionId: string;
  tokenCode: string;
  printerNumber: number;
  targetPrinterName?: string;
  hasColor: boolean;
  files: ReceivedJobFile[];
  totalAmount: number;
  paymentMethod: string;
  paymentStatus: string;
  enqueuedAt: Date;
}

export interface PrinterWorkerStatus {
  printerName: string;
  printerNumber: number;
  role: "bw" | "color" | "both" | "disabled";
  isBusy: boolean;
  currentJobToken?: string;
  currentSessionId?: string;
  jobsCompleted: number;
}

export interface TokenAllocation {
  tokenCode: string;
  printerNumber: number;
  targetPrinterName?: string;
  queuePosition: number;
}

export interface DispatcherCallbacks {
  onJobStarted?: (job: QueuedPrintJob, printerName: string) => void;
  onJobFileProgress?: (
    sessionId: string,
    fileName: string,
    fileIndex: number,
    totalFiles: number,
    printerName: string
  ) => void;
  onJobCompleted?: (job: QueuedPrintJob, printerName: string) => void;
  onJobFailed?: (job: QueuedPrintJob, error: string, printerName: string) => void;
  onQueueUpdated?: (
    queuedJobs: QueuedPrintJob[],
    workers: PrinterWorkerStatus[]
  ) => void;
}

const MAX_FAILED_JOBS_CACHE = 5;
const FAILED_JOB_TTL_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000; // 15 minutes max retention for binary buffers

export class FifoPrintDispatcher {
  private queue: QueuedPrintJob[] = [];
  private allocatedTokens: Map<string, TokenAllocation> = new Map();
  private failedJobs: Map<string, { job: QueuedPrintJob; timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> }> =
    new Map();
  private printerWorkers: Map<
    string,
    {
      isBusy: boolean;
      currentJobToken?: string;
      currentSessionId?: string;
      jobsCompleted: number;
    }
  > = new Map();

  private dailySequence: number = 0;
  private currentDateStr: string = "";
  private shopId: string = "";
  private callbacks: DispatcherCallbacks = {};

  constructor(shopId: string, initialPrintsCount: number = 0) {
    this.shopId = shopId;
    this.initSequence(initialPrintsCount);
  }

  public setCallbacks(callbacks: DispatcherCallbacks): void {
    this.callbacks = callbacks;
  }

  public setShopId(shopId: string, initialPrintsCount: number = 0): void {
    this.shopId = shopId;
    this.initSequence(initialPrintsCount);
  }

  private initSequence(initialCount: number = 0): void {
    const today = new Date().toDateString();
    this.currentDateStr = today;

    const storageKey = `printap_local_seq_${this.shopId || "default"}_${today}`;
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(storageKey);

    if (saved !== null) {
      const parsed = parseInt(saved, 10);
      const higher = Number.isFinite(parsed)
        ? Math.max(parsed, initialCount, this.dailySequence)
        : Math.max(initialCount, this.dailySequence);
      this.dailySequence = higher;
    } else {
      this.dailySequence = Math.max(this.dailySequence, initialCount);
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(storageKey, String(this.dailySequence));
      } catch {}
    }
  }

  public syncSequence(highestKnownSequence: number): void {
    this.checkDayRollover();
    if (highestKnownSequence > this.dailySequence) {
      this.dailySequence = highestKnownSequence;
      const storageKey = `printap_local_seq_${this.shopId || "default"}_${this.currentDateStr}`;
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(storageKey, String(this.dailySequence));
      } catch {}
    }
  }

  public syncFromOrders(orders: Array<{ token_code?: string | null }>): void {
    let maxSeq = 0;
    for (const order of orders) {
      if (!order.token_code) continue;
      const clean = order.token_code.replace(/^#/, "");
      if (clean.includes("-")) {
        const parts = clean.split("-");
        const seq = parseInt(parts[parts.length - 1], 10);
        if (Number.isFinite(seq) && seq > maxSeq) maxSeq = seq;
      } else if (/^\d{2,}$/.test(clean)) {
        const seq = parseInt(clean.slice(1), 10);
        if (Number.isFinite(seq) && seq > maxSeq) maxSeq = seq;
      }
    }
    const targetSeq = Math.max(maxSeq, orders.length);
    this.syncSequence(targetSeq);
  }

  private checkDayRollover(): void {
    const today = new Date().toDateString();
    if (today !== this.currentDateStr) {
      this.currentDateStr = today;
      this.dailySequence = 0;
      this.allocatedTokens.clear();
      const storageKey = `printap_local_seq_${this.shopId || "default"}_${today}`;
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(storageKey, "0");
      } catch {}
    }
  }

  public allocateToken(
    sessionId: string,
    hasColor: boolean,
    assignments: Record<string, PrinterAssignment>
  ): TokenAllocation {
    this.checkDayRollover();

    const existing = this.allocatedTokens.get(sessionId);
    if (existing) {
      return existing;
    }

    this.dailySequence += 1;
    const storageKey = `printap_local_seq_${this.shopId || "default"}_${this.currentDateStr}`;
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(storageKey, String(this.dailySequence));
    } catch {}

    const compatiblePrinter = this.findBestCompatiblePrinter(hasColor, assignments);
    const printerNumber = compatiblePrinter?.printer_number || 1;
    const targetPrinterName = compatiblePrinter?.printer_name;

    const tokenCode = `#${printerNumber}${this.dailySequence}`;
    const queuePosition = this.getEstimatedQueuePosition();

    const allocation: TokenAllocation = {
      tokenCode,
      printerNumber,
      targetPrinterName,
      queuePosition,
    };

    this.allocatedTokens.set(sessionId, allocation);
    return allocation;
  }

  public getAllocatedToken(sessionId: string): TokenAllocation | undefined {
    return this.allocatedTokens.get(sessionId);
  }

  public enqueue(
    job: QueuedPrintJob,
    assignments: Record<string, PrinterAssignment>
  ): void {
    if (this.queue.some((q) => q.sessionId === job.sessionId)) {
      return;
    }

    this.queue.push(job);
    this.notifyQueueUpdate(assignments);
    this.processQueue(assignments);
  }

  public updateJobPaymentStatus(
    sessionId: string,
    paymentStatus: string,
    assignments?: Record<string, PrinterAssignment>
  ): void {
    const job = this.queue.find((q) => q.sessionId === sessionId);
    if (job) {
      job.paymentStatus = paymentStatus;
      if (assignments) {
        this.processQueue(assignments);
      }
    }
  }

  public cancelJob(
    sessionId: string,
    assignments: Record<string, PrinterAssignment>
  ): void {
    const index = this.queue.findIndex((q) => q.sessionId === sessionId);
    if (index !== -1) {
      const [removed] = this.queue.splice(index, 1);
      removed.files.forEach((f) => {
        (f as { buffer: ArrayBuffer | null }).buffer = new ArrayBuffer(0);
      });
      this.notifyQueueUpdate(assignments);
    }
    this.dismissFailedJob(sessionId);
  }

  public processQueue(assignments: Record<string, PrinterAssignment>): void {
    if (this.queue.length === 0) {
      return;
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < this.queue.length; i++) {
      const job = this.queue[i];

      if (job.paymentStatus !== "confirmed") {
        continue;
      }

      const availablePrinter = this.findIdleCompatiblePrinter(
        job.hasColor,
        assignments
      );

      if (availablePrinter) {
        this.queue.splice(i, 1);
        i--;

        const printerName = availablePrinter.printer_name;
        const currentWorker = this.printerWorkers.get(printerName) || {
          isBusy: false,
          jobsCompleted: 0,
        };

        this.printerWorkers.set(printerName, {
          ...currentWorker,
          isBusy: true,
          currentSessionId: job.sessionId,
          currentJobToken: job.tokenCode,
        });

        this.notifyQueueUpdate(assignments);
        this.executePrintJob(job, availablePrinter, assignments);
      }
    }
  }

  private async executePrintJob(
    job: QueuedPrintJob,
    printer: PrinterAssignment,
    assignments: Record<string, PrinterAssignment>
  ): Promise<void> {
    const printerName = printer.printer_name;
    this.callbacks.onJobStarted?.(job, printerName);

    try {
      for (let fIdx = 0; fIdx < job.files.length; fIdx++) {
        const file = job.files[fIdx];
        const settings = file.settings;

        this.callbacks.onJobFileProgress?.(
          job.sessionId,
          file.fileName,
          fIdx + 1,
          job.files.length,
          printerName
        );

        const printRes = await window.electronAPI.printPdf(
          file.buffer,
          job.sessionId,
          {
            fileId: file.fileId || `f${fIdx + 1}`,
            printer: printerName,
            copies: settings.copies || 1,
            colorMode: settings.colorMode || "bw",
            duplex: settings.duplex || "single",
            orientation: settings.orientation,
            scale: settings.pageFit === "fill" ? "noscale" : "fit",
          }
        );

        if (printRes && !printRes.success) {
          throw new Error(
            printRes.error ||
              `Hardware spooler error on ${printerName || "Default Printer"}`
          );
        }
      }

      this.dismissFailedJob(job.sessionId);

      job.files.forEach((f) => {
        (f as { buffer: ArrayBuffer | null }).buffer = new ArrayBuffer(0);
      });

      const worker = this.printerWorkers.get(printerName) || {
        isBusy: false,
        jobsCompleted: 0,
      };
      this.printerWorkers.set(printerName, {
        isBusy: false,
        currentJobToken: undefined,
        currentSessionId: undefined,
        jobsCompleted: worker.jobsCompleted + 1,
      });

      this.callbacks.onJobCompleted?.(job, printerName || "Default Printer");
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const errorMsg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
      this.storeFailedJob(job);

      const worker = this.printerWorkers.get(printerName) || {
        isBusy: false,
        jobsCompleted: 0,
      };
      this.printerWorkers.set(printerName, {
        isBusy: false,
        currentJobToken: undefined,
        currentSessionId: undefined,
        jobsCompleted: worker.jobsCompleted,
      });

      this.callbacks.onJobFailed?.(
        job,
        errorMsg,
        printerName || "Default Printer"
      );
    } finally {
      this.notifyQueueUpdate(assignments);
      setTimeout(() => {
        this.processQueue(assignments);
      }, 100);
    }
  }

  private storeFailedJob(job: QueuedPrintJob): void {
    this.dismissFailedJob(job.sessionId);

    if (this.failedJobs.size >= MAX_FAILED_JOBS_CACHE) {
      const oldestKey = this.failedJobs.keys().next().value;
      if (oldestKey) {
        this.dismissFailedJob(oldestKey);
      }
    }

    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      this.dismissFailedJob(job.sessionId);
    }, FAILED_JOB_TTL_MS);

    this.failedJobs.set(job.sessionId, { job, timer });
  }

  private findIdleCompatiblePrinter(
    hasColor: boolean,
    assignments: Record<string, PrinterAssignment>
  ): PrinterAssignment | null {
    const list = Object.values(assignments).filter(
      (a) => a.role !== "disabled" && a.is_active !== false
    );

    if (list.length === 0) {
      const workerState = this.printerWorkers.get("") || {
        isBusy: false,
        jobsCompleted: 0,
      };
      if (!workerState.isBusy) {
        return {
          shop_id: this.shopId,
          printer_name: "",
          printer_number: 1,
          role: "both",
          is_default: true,
          is_active: true,
        };
      }
      return null;
    }

    const idleList = list.filter((p) => {
      const state = this.printerWorkers.get(p.printer_name);
      return !state?.isBusy;
    });

    if (idleList.length === 0) return null;

    const compatibleIdle = idleList.filter((p) =>
      this.isCompatible(p, hasColor)
    );
    if (compatibleIdle.length === 0) return null;

    compatibleIdle.sort((a, b) => {
      const scoreA = this.getCompatibilityScore(a, hasColor);
      const scoreB = this.getCompatibilityScore(b, hasColor);
      if (scoreA !== scoreB) return scoreB - scoreA;
      const countA = this.printerWorkers.get(a.printer_name)?.jobsCompleted || 0;
      const countB = this.printerWorkers.get(b.printer_name)?.jobsCompleted || 0;
      return countA - countB;
    });

    return compatibleIdle[0];
  }

  private findBestCompatiblePrinter(
    hasColor: boolean,
    assignments: Record<string, PrinterAssignment>
  ): PrinterAssignment | null {
    const list = Object.values(assignments).filter(
      (a) => a.role !== "disabled" && a.is_active !== false
    );

    if (list.length === 0) {
      return {
        shop_id: this.shopId,
        printer_name: "",
        printer_number: 1,
        role: "both",
        is_default: true,
        is_active: true,
      };
    }

    const compatible = list.filter((p) => this.isCompatible(p, hasColor));
    if (compatible.length > 0) {
      compatible.sort(
        (a, b) =>
          this.getCompatibilityScore(b, hasColor) -
          this.getCompatibilityScore(a, hasColor)
      );
      return compatible[0];
    }

    return list.find((a) => a.is_default) || list[0];
  }

  private isCompatible(printer: PrinterAssignment, hasColor: boolean): boolean {
    if (printer.role === "disabled" || printer.is_active === false) return false;
    return hasColor
      ? printer.role === "color" || printer.role === "both"
      : printer.role === "bw" || printer.role === "both";
  }

  private getCompatibilityScore(
    printer: PrinterAssignment,
    hasColor: boolean
  ): number {
    if (hasColor) {
      return printer.role === "color" ? 100 : printer.role === "both" ? 50 : 0;
    }
    return printer.role === "bw" ? 100 : printer.role === "both" ? 50 : 0;
  }

  private getEstimatedQueuePosition(): number {
    let busyCount = 0;
    for (const w of this.printerWorkers.values()) {
      if (w.isBusy) busyCount++;
    }
    return this.queue.length + (busyCount > 0 ? 1 : 0) + 1;
  }

  private notifyQueueUpdate(
    assignments: Record<string, PrinterAssignment>
  ): void {
    const workers = this.getPrinterWorkerStatuses(assignments);
    this.callbacks.onQueueUpdated?.([...this.queue], workers);
  }

  public getQueue(): QueuedPrintJob[] {
    return [...this.queue];
  }

  public getPrinterWorkerStatuses(
    assignments: Record<string, PrinterAssignment>
  ): PrinterWorkerStatus[] {
    const list = Object.values(assignments).filter(
      (a) => a.role !== "disabled" && a.is_active !== false
    );

    return list.map((p) => {
      const state = this.printerWorkers.get(p.printer_name);
      return {
        printerName: p.printer_name,
        printerNumber: p.printer_number,
        role: p.role,
        isBusy: Boolean(state?.isBusy),
        currentJobToken: state?.currentJobToken,
        currentSessionId: state?.currentSessionId,
        jobsCompleted: state?.jobsCompleted || 0,
      };
    });
  }

  public retryJob(
    sessionId: string,
    assignments: Record<string, PrinterAssignment>
  ): boolean {
    const entry = this.failedJobs.get(sessionId);
    if (!entry) return false;

    clearTimeout(entry.timer);
    this.failedJobs.delete(sessionId);
    this.queue.unshift(entry.job);
    this.notifyQueueUpdate(assignments);
    this.processQueue(assignments);
    return true;
  }

  public dismissFailedJob(sessionId: string): void {
    const entry = this.failedJobs.get(sessionId);
    if (entry) {
      clearTimeout(entry.timer);
      entry.job.files.forEach((f) => {
        (f as { buffer: ArrayBuffer | null }).buffer = new ArrayBuffer(0);
      });
      this.failedJobs.delete(sessionId);
    }
  }

  public getFailedJobs(): QueuedPrintJob[] {
    return Array.from(this.failedJobs.values()).map((e) => e.job);
  }
}

  IconLive,

export type AdminPage = "live" | "history" | "printers" | "services" | "analytics" | "qr" | "tokens" | "profile";

interface SidebarProps {
  currentPage: AdminPage;
  onSelectPage: (page: AdminPage) => void;
  isOnline: boolean;
  onToggleOnline: (value: boolean) => void;
  isTogglingOnline?: boolean;
  onLogoutRequest?: () => void;
  onHideSidebar?: () => void;
  tokenBalance?: number | null;
  tokenAlert?: { balance: number; threshold: boolean } | null;

export const Sidebar: React.FC<SidebarProps> = ({
  currentPage,
  onSelectPage,
  isOnline,
  onToggleOnline,
  isTogglingOnline = false,
  onLogoutRequest,
  onHideSidebar,
  tokenBalance,
  tokenAlert,
}) => {
  const { shop, logout } = useAuth();
  const [showOfflineConfirm, setShowOfflineConfirm] = React.useState(false);

  const handleToggleClick = () => {
    if (isTogglingOnline) return;

      setShowOfflineConfirm(true);
    } else {
      onToggleOnline(true);
    }
  };

  const navItems: { id: AdminPage; label: string; icon: React.ReactNode }[] = [
    {
      id: "live",

    {

    },
    {
      id: "printers",
      label: "Printers Control",
      icon: <IconPrinter size={18} className="sidebar-item-icon" />,
    },
    {
      id: "services",
      label: "Services & Rates",
      icon: <IconTag size={18} className="sidebar-item-icon" />,
    },
    {
      id: "analytics",
      label: "Revenue & Analytics",
      icon: <IconAnalytics size={18} className="sidebar-item-icon" />,
    },
    {
      id: "qr",
      label: "Counter QR Stand",
      icon: <IconQr size={18} className="sidebar-item-icon" />,
    },
    {
      id: "tokens",
      label: "Token Top-up",
      icon: <IconCash size={18} className="sidebar-item-icon" />,
    },
    {
      id: "profile",
      label: "Shop Profile",
      icon: <IconProfile size={18} className="sidebar-item-icon" />,
    },
  ];

  return (
    <>
      <aside className="sidebar">
        {/* Top Shop Header & Online/Offline Control */}
        <div className="sidebar-header">
          <div className="sidebar-top-shop">
            <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: 6 }}>
              <h1 className="sidebar-top-shop-name" title={shop?.name || "Print Shop"}>
                {shop?.name || "Print Shop"}
              </h1>
              {onHideSidebar && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="sidebar-collapse-btn"
                  onClick={onHideSidebar}
                  title="Hide sidebar (Alt+S)"
                  aria-label="Hide sidebar"
                >
                  <IconChevronLeft size={15} />
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Online / Offline Presence Toggle */}
          <div className={`sidebar-online-card ${isTogglingOnline ? "connecting" : isOnline ? "online" : "offline"}`}>
            <div className="sidebar-online-header">
              <div className="sidebar-online-label-group">
                {isTogglingOnline && <div className="sidebar-online-spinner-ring" />}
                <div className="sidebar-online-text">
                  <span className="sidebar-online-title">
                    {isTogglingOnline
                      ? (isOnline ? "Switching Offline..." : "Going Online...")
                      : (isOnline ? "Accepting Jobs" : "Shop Offline")}
                  </span>
                  <span className="sidebar-online-subtitle">
                    {isTogglingOnline
                      ? "Checking printers & network..."
                      : (isOnline ? "Customers can print" : "Website is offline")}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                className={`toggle-switch-btn ${isOnline ? "checked" : ""} ${
                  isTogglingOnline ? "loading" : ""
                }`}
                onClick={handleToggleClick}
                disabled={isTogglingOnline}
                aria-label={
                  isTogglingOnline
                    ? "Updating shop presence..."
                    : isOnline
                    ? "Go offline"
                    : "Go online"
                }
                title={
                  isTogglingOnline
                    ? "Please wait, updating shop presence..."
                    : isOnline
                    ? "Switch shop to offline"
                    : "Switch shop to online"
                }
              >
                <span className="toggle-switch-knob">
                  {isTogglingOnline ? (
                    <span className="toggle-spinner" />
                  ) : isOnline ? (
                    <span className="toggle-check-dot" />
                  ) : null}
                </span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Token Balance Card */}
          {tokenBalance !== undefined && tokenBalance !== null && (() => {
            const tokenStatus =
              tokenBalance === 0
                ? "exhausted"
                : tokenBalance <= 5
                ? "critical"
                : tokenBalance <= 15
                ? "warning"
                : "good";

            const tokenColor =
              tokenStatus === "good"
                ? "#10b981"
                : tokenStatus === "warning"
                ? "#f59e0b"
                : tokenStatus === "critical"
                ? "#ef4444"
                : "#dc2626";

            return (
              <div className={`sidebar-token-card ${tokenStatus}`}>
                <div className="sidebar-token-header">
                  <span className="sidebar-token-title">Token Balance</span>
                  <span
                    className="sidebar-token-count"
                    style={{ color: tokenColor, fontWeight: 700 }}
                  >
                    {tokenBalance} left
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="sidebar-token-bar-container">
                  <div
                    className="sidebar-token-bar-fill"
                    style={{
                      width: `${Math.min(100, Math.max(0, (tokenBalance / 20) * 100))}%`,
                      backgroundColor: tokenColor,
                    }}
                  />
                </div>
                {tokenBalance === 0 ? (
                  <div className="sidebar-token-alert-container">
                    <div className="sidebar-token-alert">
                      ⛔ Tokens exhausted. Top up to print.
                    </div>
                    <button type="button" className="sidebar-topup-btn" onClick={() => onSelectPage("tokens")}>
                      Top Up
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ) : tokenBalance <= 5 ? (
                  <div className="sidebar-token-alert-container">
                    <div className="sidebar-token-alert">
                      🔴 Critical low balance!
                    </div>
                    <button type="button" className="sidebar-topup-btn" onClick={() => onSelectPage("tokens")}>
                      Top Up
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ) : tokenBalance <= 15 ? (
                  <div className="sidebar-token-alert-container">
                    <div className="sidebar-token-alert">
                      🟡 Low balance — top up
                    </div>
                    <button type="button" className="sidebar-topup-btn" onClick={() => onSelectPage("tokens")}>
                      Top Up
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ) : null}
              </div>
            );
          })()}
        </div>

        {/* Clean Nav List */}
        <nav className="sidebar-nav">
          {navItems.map((item) => {
            const isActive = currentPage === item.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={item.id}
                type="button"
                className={`sidebar-item ${isActive ? "active" : ""}`}
                onClick={() => onSelectPage(item.id)}
              >
                {item.icon}
                <span>{item.label}</span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </nav>

        {/* Footer Gateway */}
        <div className="sidebar-footer">
          <button
            type="button"
            className="sidebar-logout-btn"
            onClick={() => {
              if (onLogoutRequest) {
                onLogoutRequest();
              } else {
                logout();
              }
            }}
            title="Sign out of this shop terminal"
          >
            <span>Disconnect Terminal</span>
            <IconLogout size={13} />
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      {showOfflineConfirm && (
        <div className="modal-overlay" onClick={() => setShowOfflineConfirm(false)}>
          <div
            className="confirm-dialog-card"
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <div className="confirm-dialog-title">Switch Shop to Offline?</div>
            <div className="confirm-dialog-body">
              Going offline will prevent new customers from uploading or printing from your website. Any orders already in progress will complete normally.
            </div>
            <div className="confirm-dialog-actions">
              <button
                type="button"
                className="btn-dialog-cancel"
                onClick={() => setShowOfflineConfirm(false)}
              >
                Cancel
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                className="btn-dialog-confirm"
                onClick={() => {
                  setShowOfflineConfirm(false);
                  onToggleOnline(false);
                }}
              >
                Go Offline
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
};


───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
FILE [32/46]: apps/desktop/src/renderer/components/LiveDashboard.tsx
PURPOSE: Live Monitor: Active Print Progress, Live Queue, Handover Ledger
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
import React, { useState } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import { getShopCustomerUrl } from "@printap/shared";
import {
  IconLive,
  IconPrinter,
  IconQr,
  IconCopy,
  IconCheck,
  IconChevronDown,
  IconFileText,
  IconCash,
  IconRefresh,
} from "./Icons";

export interface LiveJobFile {
  fileName: string;
  fileSize?: number;
  pageCount?: number;
  settings?: any;
}

export interface LiveJob {
  sessionId: string;
  fileName?: string;
  status: "receiving" | "saving" | "printing" | "shredding" | "done" | "error" | "cancelled" | "queued" | "pending_payment";
  progressPct?: number;
  stage?: string;
  detail?: string;
  timestamp: Date;
  tokenCode?: string;
  pickupPin?: string;
  fileCount?: number;
  files?: LiveJobFile[];
  totalAmount?: number;
  paymentMethod?: string;
  paymentStatus?: "pending" | "confirmed" | "handed_over";
  paymentConfirmedAt?: Date;
}

import type { QueuedPrintJob } from "../lib/fifo-dispatcher";

interface LiveDashboardProps {
  currentJob: LiveJob | null;
  recentJobs: LiveJob[];
  pendingQueue: QueuedPrintJob[];
  todayPrintsCount: number;
  isListening: boolean;
  connectedPrintersCount: number;
  onConfirmPayment: (sessionId: string) => void;
  onCancelOrder: (sessionId: string) => void;
  onRetryJob?: (sessionId: string) => void;
  onDismissJob?: (sessionId: string) => void;
  onNavigateToHistory?: () => void;
  tokenBalance?: number | null;
  tokenAlert?: { balance: number; threshold: boolean } | null;
}

export const LiveDashboard: React.FC<LiveDashboardProps> = React.memo(({
  currentJob,
  recentJobs,
  pendingQueue,
  todayPrintsCount,
  isListening,
  connectedPrintersCount,
  onConfirmPayment,
  onCancelOrder,
  onRetryJob,
  onDismissJob,
  onNavigateToHistory,
  tokenBalance,
  tokenAlert,
}) => {
  const { shop } = useAuth();
  const [copiedLink, setCopiedLink] = useState(false);
  // Track open/close state for filenames dropdown per sessionId
  const [expandedJobs, setExpandedJobs] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});

  const toggleJobExpanded = (sessionId: string) => {
    setExpandedJobs((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [sessionId]: !prev[sessionId],
    }));
  };

  const customerUrl = getShopCustomerUrl(shop, shop?.name || "shop");

  const handleCopyLink = () => {
    if (!customerUrl) return;
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(customerUrl);
    setCopiedLink(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopiedLink(false), 2000);
  };

  const getStatusBadge = (status: LiveJob["status"]) => {
    switch (status) {
      case "receiving":
        return { label: "Receiving Stream", className: "job-status-dot active" };
      case "saving":
        return { label: "Verifying Memory", className: "job-status-dot pending" };
      case "printing":
        return { label: "Printing to Spooler", className: "job-status-dot active" };
      case "shredding":
        return { label: "Secure Shredding", className: "job-status-dot pending" };
      case "done":
        return { label: "Completed & Shredded", className: "job-status-dot success" };
      case "error":
        return { label: "Job Failed", className: "job-status-dot error" };
      case "cancelled":
        return { label: "Cancelled", className: "job-status-dot error" };
      default:
        return { label: status, className: "job-status-dot" };
    }
  };

  const stages = [
    { id: "stream", num: "1", label: "Receive Stream" },
    { id: "verify", num: "2", label: "Memory Check" },
    { id: "spool", num: "3", label: "Spool to Printer" },
    { id: "shred", num: "4", label: "Secure Shred" },
  ];

  const getStageIndex = (status: LiveJob["status"]) => {
    switch (status) {
      case "receiving": return 0;
      case "saving": return 1;
      case "printing": return 2;
      case "shredding": return 3;
      case "done": return 4;
      case "cancelled": return -1;
      default: return 0;
    }
  };

  const activeStageIdx = currentJob ? getStageIndex(currentJob.status) : 0;
  const currentPct = currentJob?.progressPct ?? 0;

  // Filter combined jobs list for payment side (active + recent)
  const allJobsMap = new Map<string, LiveJob>();

  // 1. Put recent jobs first
  recentJobs.forEach((j) => {
    if (j.sessionId) allJobsMap.set(j.sessionId, { ...j });
  });

  // 2. Merge currentJob if present
  if (currentJob && currentJob.sessionId) {
    const existing = allJobsMap.get(currentJob.sessionId);
    const isConfirmed =
      currentJob.paymentStatus === "confirmed" ||
      existing?.paymentStatus === "confirmed";

    allJobsMap.set(currentJob.sessionId, {
      ...existing,
      ...currentJob,
      tokenCode: currentJob.tokenCode || existing?.tokenCode,
      totalAmount:
        currentJob.totalAmount !== undefined && currentJob.totalAmount > 0
          ? currentJob.totalAmount
          : existing?.totalAmount,
      paymentMethod: currentJob.paymentMethod || existing?.paymentMethod || "upi",
      paymentStatus: isConfirmed
        ? "confirmed"
        : currentJob.paymentStatus || existing?.paymentStatus || "pending",
      paymentConfirmedAt:
        currentJob.paymentConfirmedAt || existing?.paymentConfirmedAt,
      files: currentJob.files || existing?.files,
      fileCount: currentJob.fileCount || existing?.fileCount,
    });
  }

  const allJobs = Array.from(allJobsMap.values());

  const pendingPayments = allJobs.filter(
    (j) =>
      // Exclude uninitialized socket connections that don't have order details yet
      (j.tokenCode || (j.totalAmount !== undefined && j.totalAmount > 0)) &&
      (!j.paymentStatus || j.paymentStatus === "pending")
  );
  const settledPayments = allJobs.filter(
    (j) =>
      j.status === "done" &&
      (j.paymentStatus === "confirmed" || j.paymentStatus === "handed_over" || !j.paymentStatus || (j.paymentStatus as any) === "done")
  );
  // Only the last 10 are displayed on the live monitor; the rest are in History
  const displayedSettled = settledPayments.slice(0, 10);

  // Combine pendingQueue, currentJob, and recentJobs by sessionId into a single deduplicated list
  const combinedQueueJobs = React.useMemo(() => {
    const jobMap = new Map<string, any>();

    // 1. Add recentJobs first (historical/completed/cancelled)
    recentJobs.forEach((j) => {
      if (j.sessionId) {
        jobMap.set(j.sessionId, { ...j });
      }
    });

    // 2. Overlay pendingQueue (authoritative for unprinted orders waiting for payment/print)
    pendingQueue.forEach((pq) => {
      if (pq.sessionId) {
        const existing = jobMap.get(pq.sessionId);
        jobMap.set(pq.sessionId, {
          ...existing,
          ...pq,
          status: existing?.status === "printing" ? "printing" : "pending_payment",
          paymentStatus: pq.paymentStatus || existing?.paymentStatus || "pending",
          fileName: pq.files?.[0]?.fileName || existing?.fileName || `Order ${pq.tokenCode}`,
          fileCount: pq.files?.length || existing?.fileCount || 1,
        });
      }
    });

    // 3. Overlay currentJob (active streaming or printing)
    if (currentJob && currentJob.sessionId) {
      const existing = jobMap.get(currentJob.sessionId);
      const isAlreadyInPendingQueue = pendingQueue.some((pq) => pq.sessionId === currentJob.sessionId);
      jobMap.set(currentJob.sessionId, {
        ...existing,
        ...currentJob,
        status: isAlreadyInPendingQueue ? "pending_payment" : currentJob.status,
      });
    }

    return Array.from(jobMap.values());
  }, [currentJob, recentJobs, pendingQueue]);

  return (
    <div className="content fade-in">
      {tokenBalance === 0 && (
        <div className="card" style={{ marginBottom: 20, backgroundColor: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.1)', borderColor: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.2)' }}>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12 }}>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 24 }}>⛔</div>
            <div>
              <h3 style={{ margin: 0, color: 'rgb(239, 68, 68)', fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700 }}>Tokens Exhausted</h3>
              <p style={{ margin: 0, color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontSize: 13, marginTop: 4 }}>
                Your print shop is out of tokens. All incoming print jobs will be rejected. Please top up your tokens.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {tokenBalance !== 0 && tokenAlert && (
        <div className="card" style={{ marginBottom: 20, backgroundColor: 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.1)', borderColor: 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.2)' }}>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12 }}>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 24 }}>{tokenAlert.balance <= 5 ? '🔴' : '🟡'}</div>
            <div>
              <h3 style={{ margin: 0, color: 'rgb(245, 158, 11)', fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700 }}>Low Token Balance</h3>
              <p style={{ margin: 0, color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontSize: 13, marginTop: 4 }}>
                You have {tokenAlert.balance} tokens remaining. Top up soon to avoid interruption.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Page Header */}
      <div className="page-header">
        <h1 className="page-title">
          <span>Live Monitor</span>
          <span className="status-badge active">Live & Connected</span>
        </h1>
        <p className="page-subtitle">
          Real-time print queue and counter payment verification for {shop?.name || "your counter"}.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Top Stat Cards (Terminal Gateway removed -> 3 balanced cards) */}
      <div className="stats-grid">
        <div className="stat-card">
          <div className="stat-label">
            <span>Today's Total Prints</span>
            <IconPrinter size={16} color="var(--text-muted)" />
          </div>
          <div className="stat-value">{todayPrintsCount}</div>
          <div className="stat-footnote">Direct peer-to-peer queue</div>
        </div>

        <div className="stat-card">
          <div className="stat-label">
            <span>Connected Printers</span>
            <IconPrinter size={16} color="var(--text-muted)" />
          </div>
          <div className="stat-value green">{connectedPrintersCount} Ready</div>
          <div className="stat-footnote">
            {connectedPrintersCount > 0 ? "Hardware detected & ready" : "No physical hardware detected"}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="stat-card">
          <div className="stat-label">
            <span>Pending Orders</span>
            <IconCash size={16} color="var(--text-muted)" />
          </div>
          <div
            className="stat-value"
            style={{
              color: pendingQueue.length > 0 ? "var(--accent-amber)" : "var(--accent-emerald)",
            }}
          >
            {pendingQueue.length}
          </div>
          <div className="stat-footnote">
            {pendingQueue.length > 0
              ? `${pendingQueue.length} awaiting action`
              : "All clear"}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
          TWO-PANEL TABLE SYSTEM:
          1. Live Orders (Unified Print Queue & Action Required)
          2. Settled Orders (Done Payments - Table)
      ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */}
      <div className="live-monitor-tables-grid">
        {/* ─── TABLE 1: Live Orders (Unified Print Queue & Action Required) ─── */}
        <div className="monitor-table-card">
          <div className="monitor-table-header">
            <div className="monitor-table-title">
              <IconPrinter size={16} color="var(--text-primary)" />
              <span>Live Orders</span>
            </div>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: "8px" }}>
              {pendingQueue.length > 0 && (
                <span className="status-badge" style={{ background: "rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.15)", color: "#b45309" }}>
                  {pendingQueue.length} Action Required
                </span>
              )}
              <span className="status-badge active">
                {currentJob ? "1 Processing" : `${combinedQueueJobs.length} Today`}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="monitor-table-container">
            <table className="monitor-table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th style={{ width: 68 }}>Token</th>
                  <th>Document & Specs</th>
                  <th>Amount</th>
                  <th style={{ width: 150, textAlign: "right" }}>Status / Action</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {combinedQueueJobs.length > 0 ? (
                  combinedQueueJobs.map((rawJob) => {
                    const job = rawJob as any;
                    const isActive = currentJob?.sessionId === job.sessionId;
                    const isReceiving = job.status === "receiving";
                    const isFailedOrCancelled = job.status === "error" || job.status === "cancelled";
                    const isPendingPayment = (!job.paymentStatus || job.paymentStatus === "pending") && !isReceiving && !isFailedOrCancelled;
                    const docCount = job.files?.length || job.fileCount || 1;
                    const amount = job.totalAmount ?? 0;
                    const totalPages =
                      job.files && job.files.length > 0
                        ? job.files.reduce(
                            (sum: number, f: LiveJobFile) => sum + (f.pageCount || 1) * (f.settings?.copies || 1),
                            0
                          )
                        : 1;
                    const isExpanded = !!expandedJobs[job.sessionId];
                    const hasMultiFiles = job.files && job.files.length > 1;

                    return (
                      <React.Fragment key={job.sessionId}>
                        <tr className={isActive || isPendingPayment ? "table-row-active" : ""}>
                          <td>
                            <span className="token-pill">{job.tokenCode || "#--"}</span>
                          </td>
                          <td>
                            <div className="table-doc-cell">
                              <span className="table-file-title" title={job.fileName || "Customer Document"}>
                                {job.fileName || "Customer Document"}
                              </span>
                              <div className="table-file-subtext">
                                <span>
                                  {docCount} {docCount === 1 ? "doc" : "docs"} • {totalPages} {totalPages === 1 ? "pg" : "pgs"}
                                </span>
                                {hasMultiFiles && (
                                  <button
                                    type="button"
                                    className={`table-expand-btn ${isExpanded ? "open" : ""}`}
                                    onClick={() => toggleJobExpanded(job.sessionId)}
                                    title="View files"
                                  >
                                    <span>{isExpanded ? "Hide" : "Files"}</span>
                                    <IconChevronDown size={11} />
                                  </button>
                                )}
                              </div>
                              {isActive && isReceiving && typeof job.progressPct === "number" && (
                                <div className="table-progress-track">
                                  <div
                                    className="table-progress-fill"
                                    style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, Math.max(0, job.progressPct))}%` }}
                                  />
                                </div>
                              )}
                            </div>
                          </td>
                          <td>
                            <div className="table-amount-due" style={{ fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--text-primary)" }}>
                              ₹{amount.toFixed(2)}
                            </div>
                            <div style={{ fontSize: "10px", color: "var(--text-muted)", marginTop: "2px", fontWeight: 500 }}>
                              {job.paymentMethod?.toUpperCase() || "UPI"}
                            </div>
                          </td>
                          <td style={{ textAlign: "right" }}>
                            {isPendingPayment ? (
                              <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: "6px", justifyContent: "flex-end", alignItems: "center" }}>
                                <button
