import React, { useState } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import { getShopCustomerUrl } from "@printap/shared";
import { IconCopy, IconCheck, IconQr, IconShield } from "./Icons";

interface ProfileProps {
  onNavigateToQr?: () => void;
}

export const Profile: React.FC<ProfileProps> = React.memo(({ onNavigateToQr }) => {
  const { shop } = useAuth();
  const [copiedKey, setCopiedKey] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isCheckingUpdate, setIsCheckingUpdate] = useState(false);
  const [updateMessage, setUpdateMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const copyToClipboard = (text: string, key: string) => {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    setCopiedKey(key);
    setTimeout(() => setCopiedKey(null), 2000);
  };

  const customerUrl = getShopCustomerUrl(shop, shop?.name || "shop");

  return (
    <div className="content fade-in">
      <div className="page-header">
        <h1 className="page-title">
          <span>Shop Profile</span>
          <span className="tech-tag active">Verified Shop</span>
        </h1>
        <p className="page-subtitle">
          Counter metadata, UPI settlement address, and terminal identity.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Primary Information */}
      <div className="card" style={{ marginBottom: 22 }}>
        <div className="card-header">
          <div className="card-title">Identity & Details</div>
          <span className="status-badge active">● Active & Verified</span>
        </div>
        <div className="card-body">
          <div className="profile-field">
            <span className="profile-field-label">Shop Name</span>
            <span className="profile-field-value">{shop?.name || "Test Shop"}</span>
          </div>

          <div className="profile-field">
            <span className="profile-field-label">UPI Settlement VPA</span>
            <span
              className="profile-field-value"
              style={{ fontFamily: "inherit" }}
            >
              {shop?.upi_vpa || "shop@upi"}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="profile-field">
            <span className="profile-field-label">Terminal Status</span>
            <span
              className="profile-field-value"
              style={{
                color: "var(--accent-emerald)",
                fontWeight: 600,
              }}
            >
              {shop?.status ? shop.status.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + shop.status.slice(1) : "Active"} • Ready for incoming prints
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="profile-field">
            <span className="profile-field-label">Registration Date</span>
            <span className="profile-field-value">
              {shop?.created_at
                ? new Date(shop.created_at).toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
                    year: "numeric",
                    month: "long",
                    day: "numeric",
                  })
                : "September 2026"}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Customer Web URL */}
      <div className="card" style={{ marginBottom: 22 }}>
        <div className="card-header">
          <div className="card-title">Customer Print URL</div>
          <span className="tech-tag">Direct P2P</span>
        </div>
        <div className="card-body">
          <div>
            <label
              style={{
                fontSize: 12,
                fontWeight: 600,
                color: "var(--text-secondary)",
                display: "block",
                marginBottom: 6,
              }}
            >
              Customer Print URL
            </label>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
              <input
                type="text"
                readOnly
                value={customerUrl}
                className="login-input"
                style={{ flex: 1 }}
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                className="btn-ghost"
                onClick={() => customerUrl && copyToClipboard(customerUrl, "url")}
              >
                {copiedKey === "url" ? (
                  <>
                    <IconCheck size={14} color="var(--accent-emerald)" />
                    <span style={{ color: "var(--accent-emerald)", fontWeight: 600 }}>Copied!</span>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <IconCopy size={14} />
                    <span>Copy URL</span>
                  </>
                )}
              </button>
            </div>
            <p
              style={{
                fontSize: 12,
                color: "var(--text-muted)",
                marginTop: 8,
                lineHeight: 1.5,
              }}
            >
              Encoded into your physical counter QR code. Customers open this link directly in their mobile browser to send PDFs without sharing phone numbers or emails.
            </p>

            {onNavigateToQr && (
              <div style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="login-btn"
                  style={{ width: "auto", padding: "9px 20px", fontSize: 12 }}
                  onClick={onNavigateToQr}
                >
                  <IconQr size={14} />
                  <span>Open Counter QR Stand →</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Software Updates & Release Channel */}
      <div className="card" style={{ marginBottom: 22 }}>
        <div className="card-header">
          <div className="card-title">Terminal Software & Updates</div>
          <span className="status-badge active">● Production Channel</span>
        </div>
        <div className="card-body">
          <div className="profile-field">
            <span className="profile-field-label">Installed Version</span>
            <span className="profile-field-value">v0.3.15</span>
          </div>

          <div className="profile-field">
            <span className="profile-field-label">Update Status</span>
            <span
              className="profile-field-value"
              style={{
                color: updateMessage?.includes("failed") ? "var(--accent-rose, #f43f5e)" : "var(--accent-emerald)",
                fontWeight: 600,
              }}
            >
              {isCheckingUpdate ? "Checking release server..." : updateMessage || "Automatic checks active on startup"}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div style={{ marginTop: 14 }}>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="pill-btn"
              disabled={isCheckingUpdate}
              onClick={async () => {
                setIsCheckingUpdate(true);
                setUpdateMessage(null);
                try {
                  if (window.electronAPI?.checkForUpdates) {
                    const res = await window.electronAPI.checkForUpdates();
                    if (res?.isDev) {
                      setUpdateMessage("Running in development mode");
                    }
                  }
                } catch {
                  setUpdateMessage("Check failed — verify internet connection");
                } finally {
                  setTimeout(() => setIsCheckingUpdate(false), 2000);
                }
              }}
            >
              <span>{isCheckingUpdate ? "Connecting to Release Server..." : "Check for Updates Now"}</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Security Architecture Badge */}
      <div
        className="card"
        style={{
          padding: 20,
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            display: "flex",
            alignItems: "center",
            gap: 10,
            fontSize: 13,
            fontWeight: 700,
            color: "var(--text-primary)",
            marginBottom: 6,
          }}
        >
          <IconShield size={16} color="var(--accent-emerald)" />
          <span>Zero-Liability Privacy Architecture</span>
        </div>
        <p
          style={{
            fontSize: 12,
            color: "var(--text-secondary)",
            lineHeight: 1.6,
          }}
        >
          The terminal operates entirely peer-to-peer over encrypted WebRTC connections. Documents are never stored on any cloud server or accessible to third parties. Once spooled to your hardware printer, temporary memory buffers are destroyed immediately.
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
});


───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
FILE [40/46]: apps/desktop/src/renderer/components/Login.tsx
PURPOSE: Shopkeeper Counter Terminal Authentication & Password Reset
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
import React, { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import { IconShield, IconCheck } from "./Icons";
import { validateEmail } from "@printap/shared";

const SAVED_EMAIL_KEY = "printap_saved_email";

const GoogleIcon: React.FC<{ size?: number }> = ({ size = 18 }) => (
  <svg style={{ width: size, height: size, flexShrink: 0 }} viewBox="0 0 24 24">
    <path
      fill="#4285F4"
      d="M22.56 12.25c0-.78-.07-1.53-.2-2.25H12v4.26h5.92c-.26 1.37-1.04 2.53-2.21 3.31v2.77h3.57c2.08-1.92 3.28-4.74 3.28-8.09z"
    />
    <path
      fill="#34A853"
      d="M12 23c2.97 0 5.46-.98 7.28-2.66l-3.57-2.77c-.98.66-2.23 1.06-3.71 1.06-2.86 0-5.29-1.93-6.16-4.53H2.18v2.84C3.99 20.53 7.7 23 12 23z"
    />
    <path
      fill="#FBBC05"
      d="M5.84 14.09c-.22-.66-.35-1.36-.35-2.09s.13-1.43.35-2.09V7.06H2.18C1.43 8.55 1 10.22 1 12s.43 3.45 1.18 4.94l2.85-2.22.81-.63z"
    />
    <path
      fill="#EA4335"
      d="M12 5.38c1.62 0 3.06.56 4.21 1.64l3.15-3.15C17.45 2.09 14.97 1 12 1 7.7 1 3.99 3.47 2.18 7.06l3.66 2.84c.87-2.6 3.3-4.52 6.16-4.52z"
    />
  </svg>
);

export const Login: React.FC = () => {
  const {
    login,
    loginWithGoogle,
    isGoogleSigningIn,
    cancelGoogleLogin,
    reopenGoogleBrowser,
    sendResetOtp,
    resetPassword,
  } = useAuth();

  // Login form state
  const [emailInput, setEmailInput] = useState("");
  const [passwordInput, setPasswordInput] = useState("");
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [rememberEmail, setRememberEmail] = useState(true);
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Forgot Password modal state
  const [showForgotModal, setShowForgotModal] = useState(false);
  const [forgotStep, setForgotStep] = useState<1 | 2>(1);
  const [forgotEmail, setForgotEmail] = useState("");
  const [forgotOtp, setForgotOtp] = useState("");
  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmNewPassword, setConfirmNewPassword] = useState("");
  const [forgotLoading, setForgotLoading] = useState(false);
  const [forgotError, setForgotError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [forgotSuccess, setForgotSuccess] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [resendCooldown, setResendCooldown] = useState(0);

  // Load saved email on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(SAVED_EMAIL_KEY);
    if (saved) {
      setEmailInput(saved);
      setForgotEmail(saved);
    }
  }, []);

  // Cooldown countdown timer
  useEffect(() => {
    if (resendCooldown <= 0) return;
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      setResendCooldown((prev) => (prev > 0 ? prev - 1 : 0));
    }, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [resendCooldown]);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const cleanId = emailInput.trim();
    if (!cleanId) {
      setErrorMessage("Please enter your email address or shop handle.");
      return;
    }
    if (!passwordInput) {
      setErrorMessage("Please enter your password.");
      return;
    }

    setIsSubmitting(true);
    setErrorMessage(null);

    const result = await login(cleanId, passwordInput);
    if (!result.success) {
      setErrorMessage(result.error || "Terminal authentication failed.");
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    } else {
      if (rememberEmail) {
        localStorage.setItem(SAVED_EMAIL_KEY, cleanId);
      } else {
        localStorage.removeItem(SAVED_EMAIL_KEY);
      }
    }
  };

  const handleGoogleSignIn = async () => {
    setErrorMessage(null);
    const result = await loginWithGoogle();
    if (!result.success && result.error && result.error !== "Login cancelled by user.") {
      setErrorMessage(result.error);
    }
  };

  // Step 1: Send OTP for password reset
  const handleSendResetCode = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const emailCheck = validateEmail(forgotEmail);
    if (!emailCheck.valid) {
      setForgotError(emailCheck.reason || "Please enter a valid registered email address.");
      return;
    }

    setForgotLoading(true);
    setForgotError(null);

    const res = await sendResetOtp(emailCheck.normalizedEmail);
    if (res.success) {
      setForgotStep(2);
      setResendCooldown(60);
    } else {
      setForgotError(res.error || "Failed to send reset code.");
    }
    setForgotLoading(false);
  };

  // Step 2: Confirm OTP & set new password
  const handleResetPassword = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (forgotOtp.trim().length !== 6) {
      setForgotError("Please enter the 6-digit verification code.");
      return;
    }
    if (newPassword.length < 8) {
      setForgotError("Password must be at least 8 characters long.");
      return;
    }
    if (newPassword !== confirmNewPassword) {
      setForgotError("Passwords do not match.");
      return;
    }

    setForgotLoading(true);
    setForgotError(null);

    const res = await resetPassword(forgotEmail.trim(), forgotOtp.trim(), newPassword);
    if (res.success) {
      setForgotSuccess("Password reset successfully! You can now log in.");
      setEmailInput(forgotEmail.trim());
      setPasswordInput(newPassword);
      setTimeout(() => {
        setShowForgotModal(false);
        setForgotStep(1);
        setForgotOtp("");
        setNewPassword("");
        setConfirmNewPassword("");
        setForgotSuccess(null);
      }, 1500);
    } else {
      setForgotError(res.error || "Password reset failed.");
    }
    setForgotLoading(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="login-container">
      <div className="login-card fade-in">
        <h1 className="login-title">Counter Terminal</h1>
        <p className="login-subtitle">
          Sign in with your registered shop account to connect your counter terminal.
        </p>

        {/* Google Sign-In Option */}
        <div style={{ marginTop: 20 }}>
          {isGoogleSigningIn ? (
            <div className="login-waiting-card fade-in">
              <div className="login-waiting-spinner" />
              <div style={{ textAlign: "left", flex: 1 }}>
                <div style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--text-primary)" }}>
                  Authorizing with Google...
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--text-muted)", marginTop: 2 }}>
                  Check your browser tab to complete sign-in.
                </div>
              </div>
              <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 6 }}>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={reopenGoogleBrowser}
                  className="btn-ghost"
                  style={{ fontSize: 11, padding: "4px 8px" }}
                  title="Reopen login tab in browser"
                >
                  Reopen
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={cancelGoogleLogin}
                  className="btn-ghost"
                  style={{ fontSize: 11, padding: "4px 8px", color: "var(--accent-rose, #f43f5e)" }}
                >
                  Cancel
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleGoogleSignIn}
              disabled={isSubmitting || isGoogleSigningIn}
              className="login-google-btn"
            >
              <GoogleIcon size={18} />
              <span>Continue with Google</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Divider */}
        <div className="login-divider">
          <span>or sign in with password</span>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>
          {/* Email Input */}
          <div style={{ textAlign: "left", marginBottom: 6 }}>
            <label
              style={{
                fontSize: 12,
                fontWeight: 600,
                color: "var(--text-secondary)",
                display: "block",
              }}
            >
              Shop Email or Subdomain Handle
            </label>
          </div>
          <input
            type="text"
            className="login-input"
            placeholder="e.g. rahul@gmail.com or apex-prints"
            value={emailInput}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setEmailInput(e.target.value);
              if (errorMessage) setErrorMessage(null);
            }}
            disabled={isSubmitting}
            autoFocus
            required
          />

          {/* Password Input */}
          <div
            style={{
              display: "flex",
              justifyContent: "space-between",
              alignItems: "center",
              marginTop: 14,
              marginBottom: 6,
            }}
          >
            <label
              style={{
                fontSize: 12,
                fontWeight: 600,
                color: "var(--text-secondary)",
              }}
            >
              Terminal Password
            </label>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
              style={{
                background: "none",
                border: "none",
                fontSize: 11,
                color: "var(--accent-primary, #6366f1)",
                cursor: "pointer",
                padding: 0,
              }}
            >
              {showPassword ? "Hide" : "Show"}
            </button>
          </div>
          <input
            type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
            className="login-input"
            placeholder="Enter your password"
            value={passwordInput}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setPasswordInput(e.target.value);
              if (errorMessage) setErrorMessage(null);
            }}
            disabled={isSubmitting}
            required
          />

          {/* Remember Email & Forgot Password */}
          <div
            style={{
              display: "flex",
              justifyContent: "space-between",
              alignItems: "center",
              marginTop: 12,
              fontSize: 12,
            }}
          >
            <label
              style={{
                display: "flex",
                alignItems: "center",
                gap: 6,
                cursor: "pointer",
                color: "var(--text-muted)",
              }}
            >
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={rememberEmail}
                onChange={(e) => setRememberEmail(e.target.checked)}
                style={{ cursor: "pointer", accentColor: "var(--accent-primary, #6366f1)" }}
              />
              Remember Email
            </label>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setShowForgotModal(true);
                setForgotEmail(emailInput || "");
                setForgotError(null);
              }}
              style={{
                background: "none",
                border: "none",
                color: "var(--accent-primary, #6366f1)",
                fontSize: 12,
                fontWeight: 600,
                cursor: "pointer",
                padding: 0,
              }}
            >
              Forgot Password?
            </button>
          </div>

          {errorMessage && (
            <div className="login-error" style={{ marginTop: 14 }}>
              <div>{errorMessage}</div>
              {errorMessage.toLowerCase().includes("onboard") && (
                <div style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      const onboardUrl = "https://www.mellod.in/onboard";
                      if (window.electronAPI?.openExternal) {
                        window.electronAPI.openExternal(onboardUrl);
                      } else {
                        window.open(onboardUrl, "_blank");
                      }
                    }}
                    style={{
                      background: "none",
                      border: "none",
                      color: "#fff",
                      textDecoration: "underline",
                      fontSize: 12,
                      fontWeight: 700,
                      cursor: "pointer",
                      padding: 0,
                    }}
                  >
                    Open Onboarding in Browser →
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Connect Button */}
          <button
            type="submit"
            className="login-btn"
            style={{ marginTop: 20 }}
            disabled={isSubmitting || !emailInput.trim() || !passwordInput}
          >
            {isSubmitting ? "Connecting Terminal..." : "Connect Counter Terminal"}
          </button>
        </form>

        {/* Onboarding Link */}
        <div style={{ marginTop: 16, fontSize: 12, color: "var(--text-muted)" }}>
          Need to onboard a new shop?{" "}
          <a
            href="https://www.mellod.in/onboard"
            target="_blank"
            rel="noreferrer"
            style={{
              color: "var(--accent-primary, #6366f1)",
              fontWeight: 600,
              textDecoration: "none",
            }}
          >
            Register on Web →
          </a>
        </div>

        {/* Trust Badges */}
        <div
          style={{
            display: "flex",
            alignItems: "center",
            justifyContent: "center",
            gap: 8,
            marginTop: 24,
            fontSize: 12,
            color: "var(--text-muted)",
          }}
        >
          <IconShield size={14} color="var(--accent-emerald)" />
          <span>Zero Cloud Storage • Direct Peer-to-Peer</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* MODAL: FORGOT & RESET PASSWORD */}
      {showForgotModal && (
        <div
          style={{
            position: "fixed",
            inset: 0,
            background: "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.75)",
            backdropFilter: "blur(4px)",
            display: "flex",
            alignItems: "center",
            justifyContent: "center",
            zIndex: 1000,
            padding: 16,
          }}
        >
          <div
            className="login-card fade-in"
            style={{
              maxWidth: 400,
              width: "100%",
              margin: 0,
              background: "var(--surface-primary, #1e1e24)",
              border: "1px solid var(--border-subtle, #333)",
            }}
          >
            <h2 style={{ fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 700, margin: "0 0 6px 0", color: "#fff" }}>
              Reset Terminal Password
            </h2>
            <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "var(--text-muted)", margin: "0 0 16px 0" }}>
              {forgotStep === 1
                ? "Enter your registered shop owner email to receive a 6-digit reset code."
                : `Enter the 6-digit code sent to ${forgotEmail} and choose a new password.`}
            </p>

            {forgotError && (
              <div className="login-error" style={{ marginBottom: 14 }}>
                {forgotError}
              </div>
            )}
            {forgotSuccess && (
              <div
                style={{
                  background: "rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.15)",
                  border: "1px solid #10b981",
                  color: "#34d399",
                  padding: 10,
                  borderRadius: 8,
                  fontSize: 12,
                  marginBottom: 14,
                  display: "flex",
                  alignItems: "center",
                  gap: 6,
                }}
              >
                <IconCheck size={14} />
                <span>{forgotSuccess}</span>
              </div>
            )}

            {forgotStep === 1 ? (
              <form onSubmit={handleSendResetCode}>
                <input
                  type="email"
                  className="login-input"
                  placeholder="Enter registered email"
                  value={forgotEmail}
                  onChange={(e) => setForgotEmail(e.target.value)}
                  disabled={forgotLoading}
                  autoFocus
                  required
                />
                <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, marginTop: 16 }}>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="btn-ghost"
                    style={{ flex: 1, justifyContent: "center" }}
                    onClick={() => setShowForgotModal(false)}
                    disabled={forgotLoading}
                  >
                    Cancel
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="submit"
                    className="login-btn"
                    style={{ flex: 1, marginTop: 0 }}
                    disabled={forgotLoading || !forgotEmail.trim()}
                  >
                    {forgotLoading ? "Sending Code..." : "Send Reset Code"}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </form>
            ) : (
              <form onSubmit={handleResetPassword}>
                <div style={{ marginBottom: 10 }}>
                  <label style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--text-secondary)", display: "block", marginBottom: 4 }}>
                    6-Digit Verification Code
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    maxLength={6}
                    className="login-input"
                    placeholder="e.g. 123456"
                    value={forgotOtp}
                    onChange={(e) => setForgotOtp(e.target.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, ""))}
                    style={{ letterSpacing: 4, textAlign: "center", fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 18 }}
                    disabled={forgotLoading}
                    autoFocus
                    required
                  />
                </div>

                <div style={{ marginBottom: 10 }}>
                  <label style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--text-secondary)", display: "block", marginBottom: 4 }}>
                    New Password (min. 8 characters)
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    className="login-input"
                    placeholder="New password"
                    value={newPassword}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewPassword(e.target.value)}
                    disabled={forgotLoading}
                    required
                  />
                </div>

                <div style={{ marginBottom: 14 }}>
                  <label style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--text-secondary)", display: "block", marginBottom: 4 }}>
                    Confirm New Password
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    className="login-input"
                    placeholder="Confirm new password"
                    value={confirmNewPassword}
                    onChange={(e) => setConfirmNewPassword(e.target.value)}
                    disabled={forgotLoading}
                    required
                  />
                </div>

                <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", marginBottom: 14, fontSize: 11 }}>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setForgotStep(1)}
                    style={{ background: "none", border: "none", color: "var(--text-muted)", cursor: "pointer", padding: 0 }}
                  >
                    ← Change Email
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={async () => {
                      if (resendCooldown > 0) return;
                      await sendResetOtp(forgotEmail.trim());
                      setResendCooldown(60);
                    }}
                    disabled={resendCooldown > 0 || forgotLoading}
                    style={{
                      background: "none",
                      border: "none",
                      color: resendCooldown > 0 ? "var(--text-muted)" : "var(--accent-primary, #6366f1)",
                      cursor: resendCooldown > 0 ? "default" : "pointer",
                      padding: 0,
                      fontWeight: 600,
                    }}
                  >
                    {resendCooldown > 0 ? `Resend code in ${resendCooldown}s` : "Resend Code"}
                  </button>
                </div>

                <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="btn-ghost"
                    style={{ flex: 1, justifyContent: "center" }}
                    onClick={() => setShowForgotModal(false)}
                    disabled={forgotLoading}
                  >
                    Cancel
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="submit"
                    className="login-btn"
                    style={{ flex: 1, marginTop: 0 }}
                    disabled={forgotLoading || forgotOtp.length !== 6 || newPassword.length < 8}
                  >
                    {forgotLoading ? "Resetting..." : "Set New Password"}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </form>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

FILE [41/46]: apps/desktop/src/renderer/components/UpdateScreen.tsx
PURPOSE: Software Auto-Updater Modal (Download Progress & Restart)

import React from "react";

export type UpdateStage = "checking" | "available" | "downloading" | "downloaded" | "error" | "not-available" | null;

interface UpdateScreenProps {
  stage: UpdateStage;
  progress?: number;
  error?: string;
  version?: string;
  onStartDownload: () => void;
  onRestart: () => void;
  onDismiss?: () => void;
  isCompulsory?: boolean;
}

export const UpdateScreen: React.FC<UpdateScreenProps> = ({
  stage,
  progress = 0,
  error,
  version,
  onStartDownload,
  onRestart,
  onDismiss,
  isCompulsory = true,
}) => {
  if (!stage || stage === "not-available" || stage === "checking") return null;

  return (
    <div className="modal-overlay" style={{ zIndex: 10000 }}>
      <div
        className="confirm-dialog-card"
        style={{
          maxWidth: stage === "error" ? 640 : 440,
          width: stage === "error" ? "min(640px, 92vw)" : "min(440px, 92vw)",
          maxHeight: "85vh",
          display: "flex",
          flexDirection: "column",
          boxSizing: "border-box",
          borderRadius: "var(--radius-lg, 12px)",
          boxShadow: "0 24px 60px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.25)",
        }}
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        {/* State 1: Update Available (Compulsory) */}
        {stage === "available" && (
          <>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 12, marginBottom: 14 }}>
              <div
                style={{
                  width: 40,
                  height: 40,
                  borderRadius: "50%",
                  background: "#FEF3C7",
                  border: "1px solid #FDE68A",
                  display: "flex",
                  alignItems: "center",
                  justifyContent: "center",
                  color: "#D97706",
                  fontSize: 20,
                  fontWeight: 700,
                  flexShrink: 0,
                }}
              >
                !
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="confirm-dialog-title" style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 16 }}>
                  Mandatory Update Required
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--text-muted)", fontFamily: "var(--font-mono)", marginTop: 2 }}>
                  {version ? `New Version: v${version}` : "New release available"}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="confirm-dialog-body" style={{ marginBottom: 22, fontSize: 13, lineHeight: 1.55 }}>
              A required update is available for the Printap Terminal. To maintain secure counter printing, database synchronization, and encryption standards, this update must be installed to continue.
            </div>

            <div className="confirm-dialog-actions" style={{ flexDirection: "column", gap: 8 }}>
              <button
                type="button"
                className="btn-dialog-confirm"
                style={{
                  width: "100%",
                  padding: "12px 18px",
                  fontSize: 13,
                  fontWeight: 700,
                  borderRadius: "var(--radius-sm, 8px)",
                  display: "flex",
                  alignItems: "center",
                  justifyContent: "center",
                  gap: 8,
                }}
                onClick={onStartDownload}
              >
                <span>Download & Install Update</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </>
        )}

        {/* State 2: Downloading */}
        {stage === "downloading" && (
          <>
            <div className="confirm-dialog-title" style={{ fontSize: 16, marginBottom: 8 }}>
              Downloading Update Package...
            </div>
            <div className="confirm-dialog-body" style={{ marginBottom: 20, textAlign: "center" }}>
              <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: "var(--text-secondary)", marginBottom: 16 }}>
                Please keep the application open. Downloading verified files from the release server.
              </div>
              
              <div
                style={{
                  width: "100%",
                  height: 8,
                  background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.06)",
                  borderRadius: 9999,
                  overflow: "hidden",
                  marginBottom: 10,
                }}
              >
                <div 
                  style={{ 
                    height: "100%", 
                    width: `${Math.min(100, Math.max(0, progress))}%`, 
                    background: "var(--accent-emerald, #10b981)",
                    borderRadius: 9999,
                    transition: "width 0.2s ease-out",
                  }} 
                />
              </div>
              <div style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700, fontFamily: "var(--font-mono)", color: "var(--text-primary)" }}>
                {Math.round(progress)}%
              </div>
            </div>
          </>
        )}

        {/* State 3: Downloaded & Ready to Restart (Compulsory) */}
        {stage === "downloaded" && (
          <>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 12, marginBottom: 14 }}>
              <div
                style={{
                  width: 40,
                  height: 40,
                  borderRadius: "50%",
                  background: "#D1FAE5",
                  border: "1px solid #A7F3D0",
                  display: "flex",
                  alignItems: "center",
                  justifyContent: "center",
                  color: "#059669",
                  fontSize: 20,
                  fontWeight: 700,
                  flexShrink: 0,
                }}
              >
                ✓
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="confirm-dialog-title" style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 16, color: "var(--accent-emerald, #10b981)" }}>
                  Update Ready to Apply
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--text-muted)", fontFamily: "var(--font-mono)", marginTop: 2 }}>
                  Package verified
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="confirm-dialog-body" style={{ marginBottom: 22, fontSize: 13, lineHeight: 1.55 }}>
              The update has been downloaded. The terminal application will restart now to apply the updates and complete installation.
            </div>

            <div className="confirm-dialog-actions" style={{ flexDirection: "column", gap: 8 }}>
              <button
                type="button"
                className="btn-dialog-confirm"
                style={{
                  width: "100%",
                  padding: "12px 18px",
                  fontSize: 13,
                  fontWeight: 700,
                  background: "var(--accent-emerald, #10b981)",
                  borderColor: "var(--accent-emerald, #10b981)",
                  borderRadius: "var(--radius-sm, 8px)",
                  display: "flex",
                  alignItems: "center",
                  justifyContent: "center",
                  gap: 8,
                }}
                onClick={onRestart}
              >
                <span>Restart & Apply Update Now</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </>
        )}

        {/* State 4: Error */}
        {stage === "error" && (
          <>
            <div className="confirm-dialog-title" style={{ color: "var(--accent-rose)", flexShrink: 0, fontSize: 16 }}>
              Update Failed
            </div>
            <div
              className="confirm-dialog-body"
              style={{
                marginBottom: 18,
                flex: "1 1 auto",
                minHeight: 0,
                display: "flex",
                flexDirection: "column",
              }}
            >
              <div style={{ marginBottom: 8, flexShrink: 0, fontSize: 13, color: "var(--text-secondary)" }}>
                An error occurred while downloading the update:
              </div>
              <div
                style={{
                  padding: "10px 12px",
                  background: "rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.08)",
                  border: "1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.2)",
                  borderRadius: "var(--radius-sm, 8px)",
                  fontSize: 11,
                  lineHeight: 1.5,
                  color: "var(--accent-rose)",
                  fontFamily: "ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, Monaco, Consolas, monospace",
                  wordBreak: "break-all",
                  whiteSpace: "pre-wrap",
                  overflowY: "auto",
                  maxHeight: "180px",
                }}
              >
                {error || "Unknown network error"}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="confirm-dialog-actions" style={{ flexShrink: 0, marginTop: "auto", display: "flex", gap: 10 }}>
              {!isCompulsory && onDismiss && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="btn-dialog-cancel"
                  onClick={onDismiss}
                  style={{ flex: 1, padding: "10px 14px", borderRadius: "var(--radius-sm, 8px)" }}
                >
                  Skip for This Session
                </button>
              )}
              <button
                type="button"
                className="btn-dialog-confirm"
                onClick={onStartDownload}
                style={{ flex: 1, padding: "10px 14px", borderRadius: "var(--radius-sm, 8px)" }}
              >
                Retry Download
              </button>
            </div>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React from "react";

interface UnsavedChangesModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  pageName: string;
  isSaving: boolean;
  onCancel: () => void;
  onDiscard: () => void;
  onSave: () => void;
}

export const UnsavedChangesModal: React.FC<UnsavedChangesModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  pageName,
  isSaving,
  onCancel,
  onDiscard,
  onSave,
}) => {
  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="modal-overlay" onClick={onCancel}>
      <div
        className="confirm-dialog-card"
        style={{ maxWidth: 440 }}
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <div className="confirm-dialog-title">Unsaved Changes</div>
        <div
          className="confirm-dialog-body"
          style={{ color: "var(--text-secondary)", lineHeight: 1.5, margin: "12px 0 20px" }}
        >
          You have unsaved changes in <strong>{pageName}</strong>. Would you like to save your
          changes before leaving this section?
        </div>
        <div
          className="confirm-dialog-actions"
          style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "flex-end", gap: 10, flexWrap: "wrap" }}
        >
          <button
            type="button"
            className="btn-dialog-cancel"
            onClick={onCancel}
            disabled={isSaving}
          >
            Cancel
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="btn-dialog-discard"
            onClick={onDiscard}
            disabled={isSaving}
          >
            Discard
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="btn-dialog-save"
            onClick={onSave}
            disabled={isSaving}
          >
            {isSaving ? "Saving..." : "Save & Continue"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE [43/46]: apps/desktop/src/renderer/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx
PURPOSE: Global React Exception Boundary
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
import React, { Component, ErrorInfo, ReactNode } from "react";

interface Props {
  children: ReactNode;
  fallbackTitle?: string;
}

interface State {
  hasError: boolean;
  error: Error | null;
  errorInfo: ErrorInfo | null;
}

export class ErrorBoundary extends Component<Props, State> {
  public state: State = {
    hasError: false,
    error: null,
    errorInfo: null,
  };

  public static getDerivedStateFromError(error: Error): State {
    return { hasError: true, error, errorInfo: null };
  }

  public componentDidCatch(error: Error, errorInfo: ErrorInfo) {
    console.error("[ErrorBoundary caught an uncaught exception]:", error, errorInfo);
    this.setState({ error, errorInfo });
  }

  private handleReload = () => {
    window.location.reload();
  };

  private handleReset = () => {
    this.setState({ hasError: false, error: null, errorInfo: null });
  };

  public render() {
    if (this.state.hasError) {
      return (
        <div
          style={{
            minHeight: "100vh",
            display: "flex",
            flexDirection: "column",
            alignItems: "center",
            justifyContent: "center",
            padding: 32,
            backgroundColor: "var(--bg-canvas, #F0EBED)",
            color: "var(--text-primary, #16181D)",
            fontFamily: "var(--font-sans, system-ui, sans-serif)",
            textAlign: "center",
          }}
        >
          <div
            style={{
              maxWidth: 520,
              width: "100%",
              backgroundColor: "var(--bg-card, #FFFFFF)",
              border: "1px solid var(--border-subtle, #D1C8CC)",
              borderRadius: "var(--radius-lg, 2px)",
              padding: "36px 32px",
              boxShadow: "0 10px 25px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.05)",
            }}
          >
            <div
              style={{
                display: "inline-flex",
                alignItems: "center",
                justifyContent: "center",
                width: 48,
                height: 48,
                borderRadius: "50%",
                backgroundColor: "#FEE2E2",
                color: "#DC2626",
                marginBottom: 20,
                fontSize: 22,
                fontWeight: "bold",
              }}
            >
              !
            </div>

            <h1
              style={{
                fontSize: 20,
                fontWeight: 700,
                marginBottom: 10,
                letterSpacing: "-0.02em",
              }}
            >
              {this.props.fallbackTitle || "Printap Exception"}
            </h1>

            <p
              style={{
                fontSize: 13,
                color: "var(--text-secondary, #424750)",
                lineHeight: 1.5,
                marginBottom: 20,
              }}
            >
              An unexpected error occurred while rendering the interface. Your print jobs and hardware
              connections remain safe in background memory.
            </p>

            {this.state.error && (
              <pre
                style={{
                  textAlign: "left",
                  fontSize: 11,
                  fontFamily: "var(--font-mono, monospace)",
                  backgroundColor: "#F9FAFB",
                  border: "1px solid var(--border-subtle, #D1C8CC)",
                  padding: 12,
                  borderRadius: "var(--radius-sm, 6px)",
                  color: "#991B1B",
                  overflowX: "auto",
                  maxHeight: 120,
                  marginBottom: 24,
                }}
              >
                {this.state.error.message}
              </pre>
            )}

            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 12, justifyContent: "center" }}>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={this.handleReset}
                className="btn-secondary"
                style={{
                  padding: "10px 18px",
                  fontSize: 13,
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  cursor: "pointer",
                }}
              >
                Attempt Recovery
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={this.handleReload}
                className="btn-primary"
                style={{
                  padding: "10px 20px",
                  fontSize: 13,
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  cursor: "pointer",
                }}
              >
                Reload Window
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      );
    }

    return this.props.children;
  }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   PRINTAP SHOP AGENT — STRUCTURAL HIGH-CONTRAST DESIGN SYSTEM
   Base Background (#F0EBED) • Luminous Glow (#FFFFFF)
   Primary Text & Structure (#16181D) • Secondary Accent (#424750)
   Subtle Borders (#D1C8CC) • Sharp Wireframe Edges • Space Grotesk & JetBrains Mono
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

:root {
  /* ─── The Core Palette ─── */
  --bg-canvas: #F0EBED;                     /* Base Background: pale, washed-out pinkish-grey */
  --surface-glow: #FFFFFF;                  /* Surface / Glow: pure white luminous surface */
  --bg-card: #FFFFFF;                       /* Card backgrounds replicating luminous central glow */
  --bg-glass-card: #FFFFFF;
  --bg-glass-card-hover: #FFFFFF;
  --bg-glass-elevated: #FFFFFF;
  --bg-glass-subtle: rgba(240, 235, 237, 0.65);
  --bg-glass-input: #FFFFFF;
  --bg-glass-sidebar: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.94);

  /* ─── Primary Text & Structure (#16181D) ─── */
  --text-primary: #16181D;                  /* Deep near-black slate for headings, primary body text */
  --color-structure: #16181D;               /* Thick borders, solid buttons, dark structural pillars */
  --border-active: #16181D;

  /* ─── Secondary Accent (#424750) ─── */
  --text-secondary: #424750;                /* Medium charcoal for secondary text, metadata */
  --accent-secondary: #424750;              /* Hover states, subtle UI icons */
  --text-muted: #5e6470;                    /* Technical metadata */
  --text-faint: #888e99;

  /* ─── Subtle Borders (#D1C8CC) ─── */
  --border-subtle: #D1C8CC;                 /* Dusty mauve-grey divider lines and 1px wireframe containers */
  --border-subtle-hover: #16181D;           /* High-contrast state */
  --border-glass: #D1C8CC;                  /* 1px crisp architectural wireframe borders */

  /* ─── High Contrast Architectural Accents (Avoiding saturated colors) ─── */
  --accent-emerald: #16181D;
  --accent-emerald-light: rgba(22, 24, 29, 0.05);
  --accent-emerald-border: #D1C8CC;

───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
FILE [46/46]: apps/web/src/lib/customer-signaling.ts
PURPOSE: Customer WebRTC Peer: SDP Offer, Chunked File Streaming, Status Listener
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
import {
  joinSignalingChannel,
  leaveSignalingChannel,
  waitForChannelReady,
  broadcast,
  DEFAULT_ICE_SERVERS,
  waitForIceGathering,
  type SignalPayload,
  type TransferManifestOrder,
  type PrintSettings,
  type UploadedDocument,
  type QueueToken,
  type PaymentMethod,
  type PaymentStatus,
  type FinishingService,
} from "@printap/shared";
import type { RealtimeChannel } from "@supabase/supabase-js";

const CHUNK_SIZE = 64 * 1024; // 64KB chunks
const MAX_BUFFERED_AMOUNT = 1024 * 1024; // 1MB high-water mark
const LOW_WATER_MARK = 256 * 1024; // 256KB low-water mark

export interface CustomerTransferFile {
  fileId: string;
  file: File;
  pageCount: number;
  settings: PrintSettings;
}

export interface CustomerSignalingCallbacks {
  onStatusChange?: (status: string, detail?: string) => void;
  onProgress?: (pct: number, fileIndex: number, totalFiles: number) => void;
  onTokenAssigned?: (data: {
    tokenCode: string;
    printerNumber?: number;
    queuePosition?: number;
  }) => void;
  onOrderStatus?: (data: SignalPayload) => void;
  onPaymentConfirmed?: (data: SignalPayload) => void;
  onError?: (error: string) => void;
}

export type TransferStatus =
  | "connecting"
  | "connected"
  | "sending"
  | "complete"
  | "error";

export interface TransferProgress {
  status: TransferStatus;
  percent: number;
  bytesSent: number;
  totalBytes: number;
  currentFileIndex?: number;
  currentFileName?: string;
  totalFiles?: number;
  error?: string;
}

export type ProgressCallback = (progress: TransferProgress) => void;

export interface SendOrderPayload {
  shopId: string;
  sessionId: string;
  documents: UploadedDocument[];
  token: QueueToken;
  paymentMethod: PaymentMethod;
  paymentStatus: PaymentStatus;
  totalAmount: number;
  finishingServices: FinishingService[];
}

export async function computeSha256Hex(buffer: ArrayBuffer): Promise<string> {
  const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", buffer);
  const hashArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer));
  return hashArray.map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}

export async function waitForBufferDrain(dc: RTCDataChannel): Promise<void> {
  if (dc.bufferedAmount <= MAX_BUFFERED_AMOUNT) {
    return;
  }
  dc.bufferedAmountLowThreshold = LOW_WATER_MARK;
  return new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
      cleanup();
      if (dc.readyState !== "open") {
        reject(new Error("DataChannel closed while draining buffer"));
      } else {
        resolve();
      }
    }, 10_000);

    const onLow = () => {
      cleanup();
      resolve();
    };

    const onClose = () => {
      cleanup();
      reject(new Error("DataChannel closed during transfer"));
    };

    const cleanup = () => {
      clearTimeout(timeout);
      dc.removeEventListener("bufferedamountlow", onLow);
      dc.removeEventListener("close", onClose);
      dc.removeEventListener("error", onClose);
    };

    dc.addEventListener("bufferedamountlow", onLow);
    dc.addEventListener("close", onClose);
    dc.addEventListener("error", onClose);
  });
}

export async function streamOrderToShop(
  shopId: string,
  sessionId: string,
  manifestOrder: TransferManifestOrder,
  files: CustomerTransferFile[],
  callbacks: CustomerSignalingCallbacks
): Promise<{
  cleanup: () => void;
  channel: RealtimeChannel;
}> {
  const channel = joinSignalingChannel(shopId, true);
  const ready = await waitForChannelReady(channel, 5000);
  if (!ready) {
    throw new Error("Could not connect to shop signaling channel.");
  }

  const pc = new RTCPeerConnection({
    iceServers: DEFAULT_ICE_SERVERS,
    iceCandidatePoolSize: 10,
  });

  let isCleanedUp = false;
  const earlyCandidates: RTCIceCandidateInit[] = [];

  const cleanup = () => {
    if (isCleanedUp) return;
    isCleanedUp = true;
    try {
      pc.close();
    } catch {}
    leaveSignalingChannel(shopId).catch(() => {});
  };

  channel.on(
    "broadcast",
    { event: "assign-token" },
    ({ payload }: { payload: SignalPayload }) => {
      if (payload.sessionId !== sessionId || !payload.tokenCode) return;
      callbacks.onTokenAssigned?.({
        tokenCode: payload.tokenCode,
        printerNumber: payload.printerNumber,
        queuePosition: payload.queuePosition,
      });
    }
  );

  channel.on(
    "broadcast",
    { event: "order-status" },
    ({ payload }: { payload: SignalPayload }) => {
      if (payload.sessionId !== sessionId) return;
      callbacks.onOrderStatus?.(payload);
    }
  );

  channel.on(
    "broadcast",
    { event: "payment-confirmed" },
    ({ payload }: { payload: SignalPayload }) => {
      if (payload.sessionId !== sessionId) return;
      callbacks.onPaymentConfirmed?.(payload);
    }
  );

  channel.on(
    "broadcast",
    { event: "webrtc-ice" },
    async ({ payload }: { payload: SignalPayload }) => {
      if (payload.sessionId !== sessionId || !payload.data) return;
      const cand = payload.data as RTCIceCandidateInit;
      if (!cand.candidate) return;
      if (!pc.remoteDescription) {
        earlyCandidates.push(cand);
        return;
      }
      try {
        await pc.addIceCandidate(new RTCIceCandidate(cand));
      } catch {}
    }
  );

  channel.on(
    "broadcast",
    { event: "webrtc-answer" },
    async ({ payload }: { payload: SignalPayload }) => {
      if (payload.sessionId !== sessionId || !payload.data) return;
      if (pc.signalingState !== "have-local-offer") return;
      try {
        await pc.setRemoteDescription(
          new RTCSessionDescription(payload.data as RTCSessionDescriptionInit)
        );
        for (const cand of earlyCandidates) {
          try {
            await pc.addIceCandidate(new RTCIceCandidate(cand));
          } catch {}
        }
        earlyCandidates.length = 0;
      } catch (err) {
        callbacks.onError?.(
          err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to set remote answer"
        );
      }
    }
  );

  pc.onicecandidate = (event) => {
    if (event.candidate && event.candidate.candidate) {
      broadcast(channel, "webrtc-ice", {
        sessionId,
        data: event.candidate.toJSON(),
      });
    }
  };

  const dc = pc.createDataChannel("printap-transfer", { ordered: true });
  dc.binaryType = "arraybuffer";

  const transferPromise = new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    const handshakeTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
      reject(new Error("Shop terminal did not respond to P2P handshake in time."));
    }, 20_000);

    dc.onopen = async () => {
      clearTimeout(handshakeTimeout);
      try {
        callbacks.onStatusChange?.("streaming", "Streaming encrypted chunks...");

        dc.send(
          JSON.stringify({
            type: "order-manifest",
            order: manifestOrder,
          })
        );

        for (let fIdx = 0; fIdx < files.length; fIdx++) {
          const item = files[fIdx];
          const arrayBuffer = await item.file.arrayBuffer();
          const totalBytes = arrayBuffer.byteLength;
          const sha256 = await computeSha256Hex(arrayBuffer);

          dc.send(
            JSON.stringify({
              type: "file-header",
              fileId: item.fileId,
              fileName: item.file.name,
              fileSize: totalBytes,
              pageCount: item.pageCount,
              settings: item.settings,
              sha256,
            })
          );

          let offset = 0;
          while (offset < totalBytes) {
            await waitForBufferDrain(dc);
            const end = Math.min(offset + CHUNK_SIZE, totalBytes);
            dc.send(arrayBuffer.slice(offset, end));
            offset = end;

            const pct = Math.min(100, Math.round((offset / totalBytes) * 100));
            callbacks.onProgress?.(pct, fIdx + 1, files.length);
          }

          await waitForBufferDrain(dc);
          dc.send(
            JSON.stringify({
              type: "file-eof",
              fileId: item.fileId,
            })
          );
        }

        await waitForBufferDrain(dc);
        dc.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "order-complete" }));
        callbacks.onStatusChange?.("transferred", "All documents beamed to counter.");
        resolve();
      } catch (err) {
        reject(err);
      }
    };

    dc.onerror = () => {
      clearTimeout(handshakeTimeout);
      reject(new Error("WebRTC DataChannel transfer interrupted."));
    };
  });

  const offer = await pc.createOffer();
  await pc.setLocalDescription(offer);
  await waitForIceGathering(pc, 800);

  const localOffer = pc.localDescription || offer;
  await broadcast(channel, "webrtc-offer", {
    sessionId,
    data: { type: localOffer.type, sdp: localOffer.sdp },
  });

  await transferPromise;

  return { cleanup, channel };
}

/**
 * Compatible wrapper around streamOrderToShop matching previous UI callbacks.
 */
export async function sendOrderToShop(
  payload: SendOrderPayload,
  _printTicket: string,
  onProgress: ProgressCallback
): Promise<void> {
  const {
    shopId,
    sessionId,
    documents,
    token,
    paymentMethod,
    paymentStatus,
    totalAmount,
    finishingServices,
  } = payload;

  const totalBytes = documents.reduce((acc, d) => acc + d.file.size, 0);

  onProgress({
    status: "connecting",
    percent: 0,
    bytesSent: 0,
    totalBytes,
    totalFiles: documents.length,
    currentFileIndex: 1,
    currentFileName: documents[0]?.file.name,
  });

  const manifestOrder: TransferManifestOrder = {
    tokenCode: token.tokenCode,
    pickupPin: token.pickupPin,
    queuePosition: token.queuePosition,
    totalAmount,
    paymentMethod,
    paymentStatus,
    finishingServices: finishingServices?.map((f) => ({
      type: f.type,
      label: f.label,
      price: f.price,
    })),
    totalFiles: documents.length,
  };

  const files: CustomerTransferFile[] = documents.map((d) => ({
    fileId: d.id,
    file: d.file,
    pageCount: d.pageCount,
    settings: d.settings,
  }));

  try {
    await streamOrderToShop(
      shopId,
      sessionId,
      manifestOrder,
      files,
      {
        onStatusChange: (status) => {
          if (status === "streaming") {
            onProgress({
              status: "sending",
              percent: 0,
              bytesSent: 0,
              totalBytes,
              totalFiles: documents.length,
              currentFileIndex: 1,
              currentFileName: documents[0]?.file.name,
            });
          }
        },
        onProgress: (pct, fileIndex, totalFiles) => {
          onProgress({
            status: "sending",
            percent: pct,
            bytesSent: Math.round((pct / 100) * totalBytes),
            totalBytes,
            currentFileIndex: fileIndex,
            currentFileName: documents[fileIndex - 1]?.file.name,
            totalFiles,
          });
        },
        onError: (err) => {
          onProgress({
            status: "error",
            percent: 0,
            bytesSent: 0,
            totalBytes,
            error: err,
          });
        },
      }
    );

    onProgress({
      status: "complete",
      percent: 100,
      bytesSent: totalBytes,
      totalBytes,
      currentFileIndex: documents.length,
      currentFileName: documents[documents.length - 1]?.file.name,
      totalFiles: documents.length,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
    onProgress({
      status: "error",
      percent: 0,
      bytesSent: 0,
      totalBytes,
      error: msg,
    });
    throw err;
  }
}

/**
 * Request an authoritative, collision-free local token directly from the Shop Desktop App.
 */
export async function requestTokenFromShop(
  shopId: string,
  params: {
    sessionId: string;
    hasColor: boolean;
    totalAmount?: number;
    fileCount?: number;
  },
  timeoutMs: number = 3000
): Promise<QueueToken> {
  const channel = joinSignalingChannel(shopId);
  await waitForChannelReady(channel, 1200);

  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    let settled = false;
    let retryInterval: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
    let timeoutTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;

    const cleanup = () => {
      if (retryInterval) clearInterval(retryInterval);
      if (timeoutTimer) clearTimeout(timeoutTimer);
    };

    const onTokenAssigned = ({ payload }: { payload: Record<string, unknown> }) => {
      const p = payload as unknown as SignalPayload;
      if (p.sessionId === params.sessionId && p.tokenCode) {
        if (!settled) {
          settled = true;
          cleanup();
          resolve({
            tokenCode: p.tokenCode,
            queuePosition: p.queuePosition || 1,
            estimatedWaitMinutes: (p.queuePosition || 1) * 2,
          });
        }
      }
    };

    channel.on("broadcast", { event: "assign-token" }, onTokenAssigned);
    channel.on("broadcast", { event: "order-status" }, onTokenAssigned);

    const transmit = () => {
      if (!settled) {
        broadcast(channel, "request-token", {
          sessionId: params.sessionId,
          hasColor: params.hasColor,
          totalAmount: params.totalAmount,
          fileCount: params.fileCount,
        });
      }
    };

    transmit();
    retryInterval = setInterval(transmit, 250);

    timeoutTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      if (!settled) {
        settled = true;
        cleanup();
        resolve({
          tokenCode: `#${params.hasColor ? 2 : 1}1`,
          queuePosition: 1,
          estimatedWaitMinutes: 2,
        });
      }
    }, timeoutMs);
  });
}
